# Spec: sidebar alignment

- Ticket: none given.
- Status: version 9 (2026-11-11). Versions up to 7 are `accepted` (the user, 2026-11-10); phases 1–4 are implemented (2026-11-10, PR #57) and the follow-up of [§9.5](#95-follow-up-pane-rows-and-headers), decided by the user on 2026-11-11, is implemented (2026-11-11, PR #57). Version 8, the tree row states of [§11](#11-tree-row-states) (U13–U15, D19–D26, phase 5), is `accepted` (the user, 2026-11-11) and implemented (2026-11-11, PR #57); it replaces the background clause of U10 and D16. Version 9 (U16, U17, D27–D29, the phase 5 amendment of [Phases](#phases)) is `accepted` (the user, 2026-11-11) and implemented (2026-11-11, uncommitted, PR #57): it redraws the phase 5 states with Doric's paper tokens and `--sidebar-ring`. Phase 5 stays unreleased in the same pull request. The follow-up of 2026-11-11 (Thread `aria-selected`, the project buttons' and comment pin's focus outline; T-5.18–T-5.21) is implemented (2026-11-11, uncommitted, PR #57).
- Builds on: commit `bb4c2453` (`main`). Every line reference names the file as it is at that commit, except the captures under `docs/designs/sidebar-alignment/`, added for this spec, and the text added in version 8 ([§11](#11-tree-row-states), phase 5 and the entries marked "version 8"), whose line references name the files at commit `7e0237ba` (branch `feat/sidebar-alignment`), where phases 1–4 and §9.5 are built. The text added in version 9 (U16, U17, G6, D27–D29, SR23, SQ10, SQ11, the Terms entries it adds, the entries marked "version 9", the version 9 material of [§11](#11-tree-row-states) from its token table to its class sets, and the "Row actions" part of [§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions)) names the files at commit `e63bbccb` (branch `feat/sidebar-alignment`), where phase 5 is built; the version 8 text it rewrites in [§11](#11-tree-row-states) keeps its `7e0237ba` references where the element did not move, and names `e63bbccb` where it says so. Every path is written from the repository root.
- Requirements: U1–U17, set by the user (jao.scheuermann). They are not reopened here. U12 and U13 are the follow-ups the user decided on 2026-11-11 from papercraft previews in conversation; U13 replaces the background clause of U10. U14 and U15 are the user's decisions of 2026-11-11 on the stage 2 review of version 8. U16 is the user's decision of 2026-11-11 on the preview's `theme` variant; it replaces U13's color clauses. U17 is the user's answers of 2026-11-11 to the scope review of version 9 (SQ10, SQ11 and the row actions); it replaces U16's outline color.
- Visual target: the text of [Visual target](#visual-target) and the three committed captures it describes. Figma and the papercraft previews are informative. For [§11](#11-tree-row-states), the target is the text of §11 and its class sets; the preview `.doric/papercraft/previews/tree-row-states.tsx` that the user approved (its `proposed` variant in version 8, its `theme` variant in version 9) is git-ignored (`.gitignore:9`), so §11 states every class it settled ([Visual target](#visual-target)).
- Feature docs: [docs/features/ui-redesign.md](../features/ui-redesign.md), whose sidebar column and Files column this change revises. No new feature doc.
- Identifiers: U1–U17 requirements; D1–D29 design decisions (in version 9, D20 and D21 superseded in part by D27 and D28, and D23 amended by D29); T-1.1 … T-4.8, T-F.1 … T-F.4 and T-5.1 … T-5.21 and T-5.6b tests (T-5.15–T-5.17 unused: T-5.15 was dropped in version 8 and the follow-up of 2026-11-11 starts at T-5.18) (with T-2.12 in phase 2); SR1–SR23 risks; SQ1–SQ11 questions, all closed.

## Terms

Thread, root Thread, subthread, Project, host, renderer, bundle, contract (`name/vN`, minor version) and pane mean what the Terms of `docs/features/ui-redesign.md:28-44` say. The terms below are this spec's.

| Term                           | Meaning                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| The user, the reader           | The user is jao.scheuermann, who set U1–U17. The reader is anyone using Doric, as in `docs/features/ui-redesign.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Sidebar column                 | The sidebar's `SidebarContent` under its 40 px header (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/organisms/project-sidebar.tsx:492`): the search field, the Thread tree, the New Thread button and the stack panes. "Column" alone means this one.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Files column                   | The right workspace panel that shows the Files view or the Changes view (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/organisms/project-panel.tsx:125-140`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Files column trees             | The Files view's tree (`bundles/features/files/src/client/tree/files-tree.tsx:346-361`) and the Changes view's tree (`bundles/features/git/src/client/changes/git-changes.tsx:361-377`, one `ChangesTree` per repository). Both draw `FileTree` rows (`packages/desktop/src/panels/file-tree.tsx`) inside a `ScrollArea` (`packages/desktop/src/components/scroll-area.tsx`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Thread kind, kind icon         | A Thread's kind is `main` (a root Thread), `sub-thread` (a subthread the reader opened) or `sub-agent` (a subthread an agent spawned) (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/domain/thread-kind.ts:3-16`, `:34-37`). Its kind icon is the lucide icon for the kind: `message-circle`, `message-circle-plus`, `bot-message-square` (`thread-kind.ts:28-32`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| Untitled Thread, Untitled icon | A Thread whose `name` is the empty string; every surface shows it as "Untitled" (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/molecules/thread-branches.tsx:109`, `:201`; `bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/domain/workspace-tabs.ts:79`; `bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/hooks/thread-navigation.ts:22`). The Untitled icon is lucide `message-circle-dashed`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Prompt, execution, idle        | A prompt is one request a Thread's agent runs. Its execution runs from its `prompt.started` event to its result; U9's "turn" is an execution. A Thread is running while one of its prompts executes: `state === 'running'` with an `activePromptId` (`thread-branches.tsx:98-99`), both set together by the runner (`bundles/runtime/runtime/src/host/domain/runner.ts:494`). Otherwise it is idle.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Thread record                  | The runtime's public `Thread` (`bundles/runtime/runtime/src/contracts/model.ts:160-194`), which the layout reads through `runtime/contracts` (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/domain/workspace.ts:4`). The fields this spec reads: `executionStartedAt` (`model.ts:173`), `lastExecutionMs` (`:174`) and `result` (`:180`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `lastStarted`                  | The host's stored record of a Thread's latest `prompt.started`: `{ promptId, at } \| null` (`bundles/runtime/runtime/src/contracts/storage.ts:112-113`). The public record derives `executionStartedAt` and `lastExecutionMs` from it (`bundles/runtime/runtime/src/host/store/threads.ts:133-149`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Busy agent terminal            | A terminal whose `origin` is `'agent'` and whose `state` is `'starting'` or `'running'` (`bundles/features/terminal/src/client/domain/terminals.ts:6-19`). A user shell (`origin: 'user'`) is never busy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Active row                     | A Thread row that shimmers and shows the elapsed badge (U9): its Thread is running, or a descendant Thread at any depth is running, or a busy agent terminal sits under the Thread or a descendant.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Shimmer                        | The `shimmer` class on the row's name: a highlight that sweeps across the name's letters while the row is active (`thread-branches.tsx:198`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Elapsed badge                  | The active row's time at its right edge, a `role="status"` element named "Agent running" (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/molecules/row-add-action.tsx:61-64`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Badge start                    | The time an elapsed badge counts from: the start of the Thread's own latest execution ([§1](#1-the-badge-start)).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| The plus                       | The 20 px "New sub-thread in {name}" button a Thread row shows on hover or focus (keyboard focus only from phase 5, U14) when the Thread can take a subthread; it opens a draft row under the Thread (`thread-branches.tsx:108-114`, `row-add-action.tsx:80-90`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Draft row                      | The inline name field that New Thread or the plus opens in the tree (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/molecules/draft-name.tsx`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Lead slot                      | The 16 px box that leads a tree row and holds its 14 px icon (`thread-branches.tsx:175-196`, `file-tree.tsx:164-185`, `bundles/features/git/src/client/changes/changes-tree.tsx:130-141`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Hover swap                     | Today's behavior of an expandable tree row: while hovered or focused, its lead icon hides and a chevron shows, turned down while the row is open (`thread-branches.tsx:180-192`, `file-tree.tsx:171-182`, `changes-tree.tsx:134-140`). U11 removes it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| Level, depth                   | `ThreadLevel` is the list of Thread nodes under one parent with its pending draft row (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/domain/thread-tree.ts:10-21`); `threadLevel` builds the root level of a Project (`thread-tree.ts:58-69`). Depth is a row's nesting count from 0; tree guides are numbered by level 0, 1, … from the left.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ThreadRows` registration      | Rows a bundle adds under each Thread of the sidebar tree, registered with `layout.sidebar/v1.addThreadRows` (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/contracts/index.ts:291-305`). It names an element `tag`, mounted under each expanded Thread with a `thread-id` attribute; `count(threadId)` above 0 makes the Thread row expandable; the owner calls the `subscribe` listener after every change of an answer. The terminal bundle registers one, which draws the terminal rows (`bundles/features/terminal/src/client/index.ts:56-82`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Follow a Project               | The terminal client store's `follow(projectId)` starts the Project's terminal watch (`bundles/features/terminal/src/client/store.ts:150-156`). The store holds no terminals for a Project it does not follow.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Stack pane                     | A group a bundle registers through `layout.stack` (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/contracts/index.ts:331-385`): Screens (`bundles/features/screen/src/client/index.ts:35-46`) and MCP (`bundles/features/mcp/src/client/index.ts:32-42`) today.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| Threads pane                   | The layout's own registration in that stack (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/domain/sidebar-stack.ts:21-29`, registered at `bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/index.ts:91`). Its special cases in the view: the "Threads" header and "Collapse Threads" toggle drawn when another pane exists (`project-sidebar.tsx:180-205`); the `headless` case, which draws no header and keeps the pane open when it is the stack's only pane (`project-sidebar.tsx:121-139`, `:512`); and `paneAction`'s exclusion of its id (`project-sidebar.tsx:482`). This change removes it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Trailing marks                 | What a Files column row shows at its right end: a file's git letter (M, A, U, D, R), a directory's change count (`file-tree.tsx:215-223`), a repository row's line totals (`changes-tree.tsx:161-168`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Row wrapper                    | Version 8. The element that holds one tree row's guides, button and trailing items: the `group/tree-row` `div` of a Thread row (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/molecules/thread-branches.tsx:151`), of a `FileTree` row (`packages/desktop/src/panels/file-tree.tsx:321`) and of the Changes repository row (`bundles/features/git/src/client/changes/changes-tree.tsx:140`), and a terminal row's `SidebarMenuSubItem` (`bundles/features/terminal/src/client/rows/terminal-rows.tsx:73-76`), a Screens or MCP row's `SidebarMenuItem` (`bundles/features/screen/src/client/elements.tsx:161`, `bundles/features/mcp/src/client/elements.tsx:145`) and the draft row's outer `div` (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/molecules/draft-name.tsx:34-39`), which have no inner wrapper.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Row fill                       | Version 8. The background a row wrapper draws for its state: hover, pressed or selected ([§11.1](#111-row-fill)).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| Mark backing                   | Version 8. The `h-5 min-w-5` badge inside a trailing mark that paints over a name scrolled under the pinned mark (`file-tree.tsx:337`, `changes-tree.tsx:159`, at `7e0237ba`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Row control                    | Version 8. The element of a row that takes keyboard focus for the row: the `role="treeitem"` element of a Thread, `FileTree` or repository row, the button of a terminal, Screens or MCP row, and the name field of the draft row or of a row being renamed. Row actions (the plus, Stop, Remove, Retry, Sign in) are not row controls.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Row outline                    | Version 9; called the row ring in version 8. The 2 px mark that a row wrapper draws while one of its row controls matches `:focus-visible` ([§11.3](#113-keyboard-focus-and-clipping)): an outline in `--sidebar-ring` offset −2 px, so it lies in the inner 2 px of the wrapper (D28, U17); in version 8 it was an inset box-shadow ring (`ring-2 ring-inset ring-sidebar-ring`) in the same band and color. Its classes are written `has-[S:focus-visible]:…`: Tailwind's `has-[S]` variant applies a class to the element while a descendant matches the selector S (the emitted rule ends in `:has(:is(S))`). In this spec "ring" otherwise names only a `ring-*` utility, a box shadow, and "outline" a CSS outline.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| Action outline                 | Version 9. The 2 px `--sidebar-ring` outline that a row action (the plus, Stop, Remove, Retry, Sign in) draws outside its own border box while it matches `:focus-visible` (D29, [§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions)).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Selected fill                  | Version 8. The `bg-sidebar-accent` background of a selected row (Thread, `FileTree` file or terminal row).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Tab, tab owner, selected tab   | Version 8. `layout.tabs/v1` keeps one list of workspace tabs per owner: the Thread the route names (`/projects/:projectId/threads/:threadId`, `bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/domain/route-selection.ts:12`), else `project:<id>` of the selected Project (`route-selection.ts:126-133`). The owner of the current route is the active owner. Each owner's list has one selected tab; `current()` answers the active owner's selected tab, and `subscribe` calls its listener after every change of any tab list, of the route or of the workspace tree (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/services/layout-tabs.ts:136-155`; `bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/index.ts:63-79`). Both methods exist since `layout.tabs/v1` 1.1.0 (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/contracts/index.ts:99-103`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| Renderer sheet, bundle sheet   | Version 8. The window's stylesheets, loaded in this order: `desktop/ui`'s `theme.css`, the renderer sheet, then one bundle sheet per bundle that ships `src/client/styles.css`, in activation order (`app/desktop/doric-renderer/src/styles.css:1-7`; `packages/desktop/src/client/styles.ts:1-2`, `:15-27`). The renderer sheet is compiled when the app is built, from the shared components and every bundle's client sources in the repository at that time (`app/desktop/doric-renderer/src/styles.css:15-19`). A bundle sheet is compiled when the bundle is built (`packages/bundle/src/build/parts.ts:321`, `:353`, `:475-485`), served as `clientStyles` (`packages/bundle/src/loader/distribution.ts:147`) and given to every window (`app/desktop/doric/src/platform/bridge/client-routing.ts:248-260`). Tailwind emits a rule only for a class token it finds in a scanned source or in an `@source inline(…)` list, so a class written after the app was built has a rule only in a bundle sheet that scans or lists it. All sheets put their utilities in `@layer utilities`; between two rules of equal specificity, the later sheet wins. |
| Tools named                    | Base UI 1.8.0 is the component library under `ScrollArea`. happy-dom is the DOM the unit tests run in; it computes no layout. `get_metadata` is the Figma tool that reads a node's frames and positions. Papercraft is the repository's tool that previews components in a conversation ([docs/features/papercraft.md](../features/papercraft.md)); its story files live under `.doric/`, which git ignores (`.gitignore:9`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| Paper tokens                   | Doric's own palette: the `--paper-*` CSS variables defined in `packages/desktop/src/tokens/tokens.ts` and generated into `packages/desktop/src/styles/tokens.css`, each with a light value and a dark value. They sit beside the `--sidebar-*` variables that the vendored shadcn sidebar draws with (`packages/desktop/src/components/sidebar.tsx`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Ghost tokens, the ghost        | The two translucent paper tokens `--paper-ghost-hover` and `--paper-ghost-pressed`: `--sidebar-foreground` at 5.9% and 8.6% alpha ([§11](#11-tree-row-states), the token table). "The ghost" is whichever of the two a state draws.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| Dashboard, project list        | The Dashboard is the layout's overview of Projects (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/organisms/dashboard.tsx`); its project list draws one `ProjectItem` per Project (`dashboard.tsx:351`, `bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/molecules/project-item.tsx`). Both draw with paper tokens.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| Hover square                   | Version 9. The background a row action (the plus, Stop, Remove, Retry) draws while the pointer is on it, over the row's fill: `hover:bg-[var(--paper-ghost-pressed)]` (D29). Sign in has none.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Theme, light and dark          | The theme is the window's color scheme: light (`:root` in `tokens.css`) or dark (`prefers-color-scheme: dark`, or the `.dark` class on the window). It does not mean the paper tokens. The preview's `theme` variant is a variant name (next entry).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Preview variants               | The papercraft preview `.doric/papercraft/previews/tree-row-states.tsx` showed the rows in variants, each a set of colors on the same rows: `proposed`, the sidebar accent mixes the user approved in version 8 (U13), and `theme`, the paper tokens the user chose in version 9 (U16). The file is git-ignored (`.gitignore:9`); [§11](#11-tree-row-states) states every class the user settled on it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Scope review of version 9      | The review of the first draft of version 9 through the Scope lens (2026-11-11). Its findings M1 (the row actions), M2 (what is copied from the Dashboard) and N1–N4 are answered in place; the user's answers to its questions are U17. The stage 2 review (Comprehension, Critique, Structural and Style) followed it ([Revisions](#revisions)).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

## Visual target

The reference is this text and three captures of the Figma file `ITniMiH6BnZHhuX6NkJhqP`, taken on 2026-11-10 and committed with this spec:

- `docs/designs/sidebar-alignment/sidebar-column.png` (node `266:22228`, 280×1078 including the 40 px window header).
- `docs/designs/sidebar-alignment/files-view.png` (node `266:21740`, the Files view at 328 px).
- `docs/designs/sidebar-alignment/changes-view.png` (node `266:22012`, the Changes view at 328 px).

Sidebar column, top to bottom:

- The search field "Search Threads", 268×36, 6 px from each side.
- 12 px, then the Thread tree. Rows are 28 px tall with a 16 px lead slot holding a 14 px icon. Each level indents 20 px; a vertical guide marks each ancestor level. Root rows wear their kind icon; an Untitled Thread wears the Untitled icon. Terminal rows ("npm run dev") wear the terminal icon in the same lead slot. No row shows a repository mark.
- An active row's name shimmers and its elapsed badge ("05:12") sits at the row's right edge. Its active child shows its own badge ("02:00").
- 12 px under the last row, the New Thread button: 268×28, secondary background, a 14 px plus icon and no text.
- Empty space down to the stack panes, which sit at the bottom with no dividers. Each pane header holds a chevron, the title (Screens, MCP) and the pane's plus action, which shows only while the header is hovered or focused. The capture `sidebar-column.png` draws it always shown; this spec departs from it by the user's decision on the papercraft preview (2026-11-11, U12, [§9.5](#95-follow-up-pane-rows-and-headers)), as SQ1 does for the header height. Under the last header, 14 px.
- The captures draw the pane headers 20 px tall. This spec keeps them 28 px (`h-7`), as decided in SQ1.

Files column trees (both captures): rows 28 px tall, 20 px per level with guides, folder and open-folder icons for directories, a file icon for files, the git letter or change count 8 px from the right edge in its status color, and a selected row's background spanning the row. The captures draw that background under the mark. Phases 1–4 end it where the mark begins, a trade-off the user accepted ([§10.2](#102-rows)); phase 5 runs it under the mark, as the captures draw it ([§11](#11-tree-row-states), U13). The captures do not show horizontal scrolling; [§10](#10-files-column-horizontal-scrolling) specifies it.

Informative only: Figma `266:22228` with its frames `266:22224` (column), `266:22226` (New Thread at y 284), `429:97567` (spacer) and `429:97568` (panes at y 984), read with `get_metadata` on 2026-11-10; and the papercraft story file `.doric/papercraft/drafts/sidebar-tree.stories.tsx`, whose `Comparison` story (variant "C") and `HoverStatesGrid` story (column "Hover · proposta (sem chevron)") the user reviewed.

Tree row states (phase 5): the text and class sets of [§11](#11-tree-row-states) are the target. The approved preview is git-ignored and informative; none of the three captures shows hover, pressed or focus states.

## Problem

The sidebar column differs from the visual target:

- The Thread tree sits in a resizable panel group with a "Threads" header and a collapse toggle whenever another pane is registered (`project-sidebar.tsx:506-519`, `:180-205`).
- The New Thread control is a text row above the tree (`project-sidebar.tsx:246-261`, `:502-504`).
- Screens and MCP are resizable panels with handles between them (`project-sidebar.tsx:509`, `:160-178`).
- Thread rows draw a repository mark at their trailing edge (`row-add-action.tsx:74-79`) and reserve 32 px for it at rest (`thread-branches.tsx:164-168`).
- Tree levels step 12 px (`packages/desktop/src/panels/tree-guides.tsx:5`); the user chose 20 px (U6).
- Terminal rows draw their icon as a direct 16 px child of the button (`bundles/features/terminal/src/client/rows/terminal-rows.tsx:97`, sized by `[&>svg]:size-4` in `packages/desktop/src/components/sidebar.tsx:701`), while Thread rows use a lead slot.
- An Untitled Thread wears its kind icon (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/molecules/thread-mark.tsx:37-56`).
- Expandable rows have the Hover swap; the user chose to keep the lead icon (U11).
- A parent Thread shows no elapsed badge and no shimmer while its children work. When a parent spawns subthreads, its own prompt often ends before theirs: a child's result is delivered into the parent's running prompt, or queued when the parent is idle (`runner.ts:244-274`, `:997-1018`). In the second case the parent sits idle while its children run. The row reads only `executionStartedAt` (`thread-branches.tsx:218`), which the host fills only while the Thread runs (`threads.ts:133-140`).

The Files column trees cannot scroll sideways: both hosts force `overflow-x-hidden!` on the scroll viewport (`files-tree.tsx:347`, `git-changes.tsx:362`), `ScrollArea` draws only a vertical scrollbar (`scroll-area.tsx:21`), each row clips (`file-tree.tsx:205`, `changes-tree.tsx:147`) and each name truncates (`file-tree.tsx:188`, `changes-tree.tsx:142`). With 20 px steps a deep path loses most of its name.

Version 8. The rows of the column and the Files column react to the pointer and the keyboard in four different ways at `7e0237ba` ([§11](#11-tree-row-states)): unselected Thread, `FileTree` and repository rows draw no hover or pressed fill; terminal rows fill in `--sidebar-accent` on hover; Screens and MCP rows fill in `--sidebar-accent` on hover and on any focus, so a clicked row stays filled after the pointer leaves; the draft row draws nothing. A selected `FileTree` row's fill stops where its mark begins, the mark backing is translucent over a scrolled name, and keyboard focus is an outer ring that the nested Thread levels clip at both sides. Row actions and status text also reveal on any focus, so they stay shown on a clicked row.

Version 9. Phase 5, built at `e63bbccb`, draws the row states with the vendored sidebar's tokens: hover and pressed fills that mix `--sidebar-accent` into `--sidebar`, and keyboard focus as a `ring-2` box shadow. The Dashboard and the project list draw with paper tokens: a row's hover is `--paper-ghost-hover` (`project-item.tsx:74`) and keyboard focus is a 2 px outline (`project-item.tsx:83-84`, `packages/desktop/src/panels/tab-strip.tsx:27`). The user compared the two on the preview and chose the paper tokens (U16). A box-shadow ring is also not drawn in forced-colors mode, while an outline is (CSS Color Adjustment Level 1, §3.1). On the paper fills, the row actions' default hover square (`--sidebar-accent`) would be lighter than a hovered row in the light theme and darker in the dark one, at 1.04:1 ([§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions)).

## Goals

- G1. The sidebar column matches U1–U8, U11 and the [Visual target](#visual-target).
- G2. A Thread row is active while its subtree works (U9), counting from the badge start.
- G3. These rules are pure functions with unit tests, and the components draw what they return: `threadActivity` and `isThreadRunning` ([§2](#2-the-activity-rule)), the badge start ([§1](#1-the-badge-start)), `elapsedBadge` and `threadRowPadding` ([§4](#4-thread-rows)), `threadRowsBusy` ([§3](#3-the-busy-answer-layoutsidebarv1-110)), `isAgentBusy` ([§3](#3-the-busy-answer-layoutsidebarv1-110)), `isUntitled` ([§5](#5-the-untitled-icon)) and `indentation` ([§7](#7-tree-indentation)).
- G4. Every name in the Files column trees, including a repository row's label, can be read whole by scrolling sideways, with the trailing marks in view (U10).
- G5. The rows U13 names (Thread and subthread rows, the Files and Changes tree rows, the Screens and MCP pane rows, the draft row and terminal rows) show hover, pressed and keyboard focus, and the Thread, `FileTree` file and terminal rows also show selection, with one fill across the whole row, under the trailing mark where the row has one; a Thread or terminal row being renamed shows the row outline (U13). Row actions and status reveals follow the pointer and keyboard focus only (U14).
- G6. The states of G5 and the row actions follow the color rule of [§11](#11-tree-row-states) (U16, U17): hover and pressed fills and the actions' hover squares in the ghost tokens, the selected fill in `--sidebar-accent`, an opaque mark backing equal to its row in every state, and every focus outline 2 px of `--sidebar-ring`, inset on rows and outside the actions. Checked by T-5.1–T-5.6, T-5.9–T-5.13 (the class tokens) and the [1:1 verification](#11-verification) (the colors, against the tables of §11 and §11.4).

## Non-goals

- Any runtime change. The Thread record already carries what the elapsed badge needs ([§1](#1-the-badge-start)).
- A "name being generated" state. New Threads are created Untitled.
- Persisting a stack pane's open state; it stays in memory (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/stores/stack.ts:13-25`).
- Changing `cwdRepo` (U5). It stays in the record; no sidebar surface reads it after this change.
- In phases 1–4, changing the rows' hover background (`rowInteraction`, `tree-guides.tsx:19-22` at `bb4c2453`, `:19-23` at `7e0237ba`). Version 8 changes it in phase 5 (U13, [§11](#11-tree-row-states)); `rowInteraction` itself stays unchanged (D22).
- In phase 5, interaction states for the skeleton rows (Thread tree and MCP pane): they are placeholders while loading, not controls.
- Changing what a stack pane draws inside it.
- In the phase 5 amendment (version 9):
  - changing the defaults of `packages/desktop/src/components/sidebar.tsx` or `button.tsx` ([§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions));
  - changing any token value in `packages/desktop/src/tokens/tokens.ts` or `tokens.css`, `--paper-focus` included (SQ10);
  - a selected fill that differs from `--sidebar` more than the hover fill (SQ11, kept);
  - the focus outline of the project list's buttons and the comment pin, which pair `outline-none` with `focus-visible:outline-2` and so draw none (`project-item.tsx:83-84`, `packages/desktop/src/comment-pin.tsx:27`): a separate fix (the user, 2026-11-11), since done in the follow-up of 2026-11-11, which adds `focus-visible:outline-solid` to them (T-5.20 and T-5.21; Revisions);
  - the pane header's `headerTag` reveal (SQ7).

## Requirements

- **U1** — Remove the Threads pane header (label, chevron, "Collapse Threads"); Threads is no longer a collapsible pane.
- **U2** — The New Thread button moves below the Thread tree: full width, icon-only (`PlusIcon` 14 px), secondary style, `h-7` (since the follow-up of 2026-11-12 its colors are the ghost tokens: `--paper-ghost-hover` at rest, `--paper-ghost-pressed` under the pointer); it keeps the accessible name "New Thread" and stays disabled without a Project.
- **U3** — The search field keeps "Search Threads".
- **U4** — Stack panes (Screens, MCP) keep their header and collapse toggle, lose the `ResizableHandle` dividers and resizing, and are pinned to the bottom of the column. An open pane takes its content height up to 40% of the column and scrolls beyond; the Thread tree takes the rest and scrolls.
- **U5** — Remove the repository marks from Thread rows; the name runs to the right edge, or up to the elapsed badge. `cwdRepo` stays in the data model.
- **U6** — Tree indentation steps 20 px per level for every tree sharing `packages/desktop/src/panels/tree-guides.tsx`; guides at 14 + 20·level px.
- **U7** — Terminal rows use the Thread rows' icon pattern: a 16 px lead slot holding a 14 px icon.
- **U8** — An Untitled Thread shows the Untitled icon instead of its kind icon in the sidebar row, the breadcrumb and the tabs.
- **U9** — A Thread row shows the elapsed badge and the shimmer while the Thread runs or any descendant, at any depth, works: a running descendant Thread, or a busy agent terminal under the Thread or a descendant. User shells do not count. Agent background terminals that never end (dev servers, watchers, `timeout_ms: 0`) keep counting while they run (decision D-A, 2026-11-10). The time counts from the start of the Thread's own last execution.
- **U10** — The Files column trees scroll horizontally. The trailing marks stay pinned to the visible right edge with `position: sticky` while the tree scrolls, and a decorated row's hover and selected background ends where its mark begins, while an undecorated row's spans the row (D16). It stays in this spec by the user's decision. Version 8: U13 replaces the background clause from phase 5 on; the row's fill runs under its mark.
- **U11** — Remove the Hover swap: on hover or focus an expandable row keeps its lead icon. Rows open and close as today, by click and keyboard. No chevron in these trees. Thread rows with children gain `aria-expanded` (SQ2); the rest of accessibility stays as it is.
- **U12** — Follow-up (user decision on the papercraft preview, 2026-11-11): Screens and MCP rows align their icons with the pane header chevrons; row actions show only on row hover or focus, over the row's right edge, without reserved space; MCP "Sign in" stays visible as a link; an empty open pane says so; a pane header's action shows only on header hover or focus ([§9.5](#95-follow-up-pane-rows-and-headers)).
- **U13** — Follow-up (user decision on the papercraft preview `tree-row-states.tsx`, 2026-11-11; `accepted` in version 8): Thread and subthread rows, the Files tree rows and the Changes tree rows get hover, pressed, selected and keyboard-focus states. The fill belongs to the whole row, not the row button, so it runs under the pinned mark. Fills are solid: hover `color-mix(in oklch, var(--sidebar-accent) 50%, var(--sidebar))`, pressed the same at 80%, selected `bg-sidebar-accent`, kept on hover; the row is rounded 4 px (`rounded-[4px]`). The mark's backing takes the row's solid color: `bg-sidebar` at rest, the hover and pressed mixes, the accent when selected. The row button draws no fill and no ring; keyboard focus is an inset 2 px `ring-sidebar-ring` on the row. A running Thread's shimmer, elapsed badge and the plus replacing it on hover or focus are unchanged ([§11](#11-tree-row-states)). Widened the same day (SQ3–SQ5): the Screens and MCP pane rows and the draft row take the same hover, pressed and focus states; none of them has a selection, so none has a selected state (SQ3); terminal rows take the same hover, pressed and focus states, and the selected fill while their terminal's tab is their Thread's selected tab (SQ4); a Thread or terminal row being renamed shows the row outline while its name field holds focus, as the draft row does (SQ5). Version 9: U16 replaces the colors of the hover and pressed fills, of the mark backing and of the ring; the structure stated here (whole-row fill on the wrapper, selected fill kept on hover, 4 px radius, opaque mark backing, no fill or ring on the button, inset focus mark) stays.
- **U14** — Follow-up (user decision on the stage 2 review of version 8, 2026-11-11; `accepted`): row actions and status reveals react to the pointer and to keyboard focus only. Remove and Retry on Screens and MCP rows, the fading status text, MCP "Sign in"'s shift, the Thread row's plus and elapsed-badge swap and the padding that makes room for the plus move from `group-focus-within/…` to a `:focus-visible` condition, so a row clicked with the mouse returns to rest when the pointer leaves. Hover still reveals them ([§11.7](#117-reveals-on-keyboard-focus-only)).
- **U15** — Follow-up (user decision on the stage 2 review of version 8, 2026-11-11; `accepted`): the screen and mcp bundles each ship a minimal bundle sheet that holds only their own classes: it compiles only its bundle's listed phase 5 classes (`source(none)` and one `@source inline('…')`) and weighs a few KB, not a full utility sheet. The spec states its cascade constraint and its size ([§11.6](#116-pane-rows-and-the-draft-row)). Stage 3 (2026-11-11): asked again, the user kept the minimal sheets rather than adding the screen and mcp client sources to the `@source` lines of the files, git and terminal sheets. The cascade check of §11.6 then found conflicts in the scan, so each sheet lists its bundle's phase 5 classes instead of scanning them; the user kept the lists (SQ9, closed).
- **U16** — Follow-up (user decision on the `theme` variant of the papercraft preview `tree-row-states.tsx`, 2026-11-11; version 9): the phase 5 row states use Doric's paper tokens, the token family of the Dashboard and the project list, instead of the sidebar accent mixes ([§11](#11-tree-row-states) says which parts copy existing code and which are new). Hover fill `bg-[var(--paper-ghost-hover)]` and pressed fill `bg-[var(--paper-ghost-pressed)]`, both translucent; keyboard focus an inset outline `outline-2 -outline-offset-2`, on the same selectors as the version 8 ring, rename and draft fields included, drawn in `--paper-focus` in the variant (U17 replaces that color); selected fill unchanged, `bg-sidebar-accent`. The mark backing stays opaque: `bg-sidebar` at rest, the ghost color layered over `--sidebar` under hover and press, `bg-sidebar-accent` when selected. The plus takes `hover:bg-[var(--paper-ghost-pressed)]` ([§11](#11-tree-row-states)).
- **U17** — Follow-up (user answers of 2026-11-11 to the scope review of version 9; version 9). The user's words were "follow the theme in everything"; this spec applies them as three decisions. (1) The row actions take the rows' version 9 look: the plus, Screens' Retry and Remove, MCP's Remove and Stop draw their hover square in `--paper-ghost-pressed`, and those five and MCP's Sign in show a 2 px focus outline in the rows' outline color under keyboard focus ([§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions) says how it combines with each action's own ring). (2) SQ10, answer (b): the outline color is `--sidebar-ring` (light `#8a7f70`, dark `#d9cdb6`), for the rows and the actions; `--paper-focus` is not used. (3) SQ11, answer (a): the selected fill stays `bg-sidebar-accent`, although the hover fill differs from `--sidebar` more (light 1.12:1 against 1.07:1, dark 1.16:1 against 1.11:1) ([§11](#11-tree-row-states)).

Which surfaces each requirement changes:

| Requirement    | Sidebar column                                | Files view     | Changes view                        | Other                                |
| -------------- | --------------------------------------------- | -------------- | ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------ |
| U1, U2, U3, U4 | yes                                           | —              | —                                   | `layout.stack` contract              |
| U5, U9         | Thread rows                                   | —              | —                                   | `layout.sidebar/v1`, terminal bundle |
| U6             | Thread and terminal rows                      | rows           | rows                                | skeleton rows                        |
| U7             | terminal rows                                 | —              | —                                   | —                                    |
| U8             | Thread rows                                   | —              | —                                   | breadcrumb, conversation tabs        |
| U10            | —                                             | yes            | yes                                 | `ScrollArea`                         |
| U11            | Thread rows                                   | directory rows | repository and directory rows       | —                                    |
| U12            | Screens and MCP rows and pane headers         | —              | —                                   | —                                    |
| U13            | Thread, terminal, draft, Screens and MCP rows | rows           | repository, directory and file rows | `FileTree` (`desktop/ui/panels`)     |
| U14            | Thread, terminal, Screens and MCP row actions | —              | —                                   | —                                    |
| U15            | —                                             | —              | —                                   | screen and mcp bundle sheets         |
| U16            | the rows and row actions of U13 and U14       | rows           | repository, directory and file rows | screen and mcp bundle sheets         |
| U17            | the rows and row actions of U13 and U14       | rows           | repository, directory and file rows | screen and mcp bundle sheets         |

## Design decisions

Each decision is detailed in the section it names. D1–D18 are numbered in section order; D19–D26 (version 8) and D27–D29 (version 9) are numbered in the order they were decided, and the Section column places each one. A superseded decision keeps its row and names what replaces it.

| Id  | Decision                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Section                                                                                                                                         |
| --- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| D1  | An idle Thread's badge start is `result.at − lastExecutionMs`, derived in the client; the runtime does not change.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | [§1](#1-the-badge-start)                                                                                                                        |
| D2  | An active Thread with no badge start shimmers, shows no time, and keeps a hidden "Agent running" status.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | [§1](#1-the-badge-start), [§4](#4-thread-rows)                                                                                                  |
