Step 2 gives them these Q rules of the same weight that sort after them: `[&:has(:focus-visible)]:outline-2` (against `focus-visible:outline-2` and `focus-visible:outline-solid`), `focus-visible:outline-ring`, `dark:outline-white/5` and `[&:has(:focus-visible)]:outline-[var(--paper-focus)]` (against `focus-visible:outline-[var(--sidebar-ring)]`). Before phase 5, `focus-visible:outline-2` is already on ten elements (`project-item.tsx:83-84`, `dashboard.tsx:369`, `:379`, `:398`, `:436`, `project-tabs.tsx:363`, `bundles/features/usage/src/client/components/thread-usage.tsx:170`, `packages/desktop/src/comment-pin.tsx:27`, `tab-strip.tsx:220`), so the re-emitted rule reaches them. One of them carries a Q: the project tab joins `compactTabClassName` (`tab-strip.tsx:27`) with `focus-visible:outline-2` (`:220`), so it carries `[&:has(:focus-visible)]:outline-2` as well, the thirteenth pair. Both rules set `outline-style: var(--tw-outline-style)` and `outline-width: 2px`, so their order changes nothing. No element carries `focus-visible:outline-solid` or `focus-visible:outline-[var(--sidebar-ring)]` before phase 5, and the six actions that take them carry none of these Q classes. The actions' own `focus-visible:ring-2` sets `box-shadow`, which no outline token sets, and only Stop, whose classes the lists do not hold, carries `focus-visible:ring-0`; the listed `focus-visible:ring-0` is the row buttons', which `cn` has cleared of `focus-visible:ring-2` ([§11.1](#111-row-fill)), so the actions add no pair to those of `focus-visible:ring-0` above.

  Not a pair, by specificity: the no-fill button's `bg-transparent` (one class) against Toggle's `data-[state=on]:bg-muted` (`packages/desktop/src/components/toggle.tsx:6`, `:10`; a class and an attribute). Q is more specific, so it wins in either order.

  T-5.12 pins each built sheet to its list, so a change to a list fails it until the check is rerun and the test updated (SR20).

The draft row. `DraftName`'s outer `div` (`draft-name.tsx:34-39`) is its row wrapper; it holds the guides, the leading icon (the Project avatar or a message icon, `draft-name.tsx:41-45`) and the name field (`EditableName`, a `role="textbox"` `contentEditable` span while editing, `editable-name.tsx:135-151`).

- It has no selection (it names something that does not exist yet) and no button.
- It keeps its classes (`relative flex min-h-7 w-full items-center gap-2 pr-8 text-xs`) and takes the unselected fill and the textbox outline of [§11](#11-tree-row-states).
- The field is focused when the draft opens (`editable-name.tsx:68-70`), and leaving it cancels the draft (`:124-129`), so the draft row holds focus for as long as it exists. An editable element matches `:focus-visible` when focused (the Selectors Level 4 heuristic quoted in [§11.3](#113-keyboard-focus-and-clipping)), so the row outline shows for the draft's whole life, beside the field's underline. Pressing inside the field (to place the caret) shows the pressed fill while the button is down.
- A Thread or terminal row being renamed draws the same row outline while its field holds focus (D25, [§11.3](#113-keyboard-focus-and-clipping)).

#### 11.7 Reveals on keyboard focus only

D26 (U14). Every reveal that today reacts to `group-focus-within/G` reacts instead to `group-has-[:focus-visible]/G`: while the group G is hovered, or while any element inside it matches `:focus-visible`. A row clicked with the mouse leaves its button focused but not `:focus-visible`, so its actions hide, its status shows and its padding returns to rest once the pointer leaves. Tab onto the row control or onto an action reveals them, as today.

- The class: `group-has-[:focus-visible]/G:X` in place of each `group-focus-within/G:X`, the "keyboard reveal on G" set of [§11](#11-tree-row-states). Tailwind 4.3.3 emits it as `.group-has-\[\:focus-visible\]\/G\:X:is(:where(.group\/G):has(:is(:focus-visible)) *)` (compiled on 2026-11-11), whose specificity (two classes' worth, from the class and from `:focus-visible` inside `:has()`) is the same as the `group-hover/G:X` rule it sits beside.
- Where: the Thread row's elapsed badge and plus (`row-add-action.tsx:56-60`, `:69-71`; `pointer-events-auto` and `opacity-100` and `opacity-0` alike) and `threadRowPadding`'s third value (`sidebar.ts:22`), on `tree-row`; Stop (`terminal-rows.tsx:141`, and the story stand-in's, `bundles/desktop/layout/stories/support/terminal-rows.tsx:57`), on `terminal`; `ON_HOVER` (`screen/…/elements.tsx:137-138`, `mcp/…/elements.tsx:115-116`), the status text (`screen/…/elements.tsx:173`, `mcp/…/elements.tsx:155`) and Sign in's `right-7` (`mcp/…/elements.tsx:166`), on `menu-item`. Stop is a row action, so U14 covers it.
- A rename field and the draft's name field match `:focus-visible` while focused ([§11.3](#113-keyboard-focus-and-clipping)), so a Thread row being renamed still shows the plus, as today; under the fallback of §11.3 it shows the plus only while hovered.
- Not changed: the pane header's `headerTag` reveal (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/organisms/project-sidebar.tsx:155`, `group-focus-within/pane-header`) is a header, not a row, and keeps reacting to any focus: the default of SQ7, which the user did not answer.
- Mixed versions: the new tokens reach a window like the other phase 5 classes ([§11.5](#115-where-the-classes-live-and-mixed-versions)): the layout's through the git and terminal sheets (and the files sheet when that bundle was built with phase 5), the terminal's through its own sheet, screen's and mcp's through their minimal sheets. On an older app with an older bundle, the reveal stays on any focus.

## Flows

**A parent waits for its children.** The reader prompts root Thread P at t0; P's row counts from `executionStartedAt` = t0. P spawns subthread C and its prompt ends at t1: P's record has `result.at` = t1 and `lastExecutionMs` = t1 − t0, so P's badge start stays t0. C runs: `threadActivity` marks C (from C's `executionStartedAt`) and P (from t0). C finishes; its result is queued into P (`runner.ts:244-274`). P starts again at t2 and counts from t2. When P ends and no descendant works, P has no entry and the badge and shimmer go.

**The reader stops the parent.** The reader stops P while C runs, and the stop pauses P's prompt: no result is written, so P keeps its shimmer and its hidden "Agent running" status and shows no time (D2). When the reader resumes P, its prompt starts again and P counts from the resume.

**An agent terminal outlives its prompt.** Agent subthread A starts a dev server in the background with `timeout_ms: 0`, and its prompt ends. The terminal store applies the update and notifies; the layout re-renders; `busy(A)` answers true; A and every ancestor are active, each counting from its own badge start. Days later the badge reads `3d 4h`. When the server exits, `busy(A)` turns false.

**A collapsed subtree.** The reader collapses P. Its rows are not drawn, so `count` is not called for them; `busy` still is, and P stays active while a hidden descendant works.

**A deep file.** The Files view shows a file eight levels down. The tree grows wider than the column and a horizontal scrollbar appears. The reader scrolls right and reads the whole name while its letter stays 8 px from the visible right edge.

**Pointing and tabbing through the trees (phase 5).** The pointer enters an unselected Thread row: the row takes the hover fill (`--paper-ghost-hover`, version 9) and, if the Thread can take a subthread, the plus shows; the pointer over the plus shows its own `--paper-ghost-pressed` square; a press takes the pressed fill; on release the Thread is selected and the row keeps the selected fill while the pointer is still over it. The pointer leaves: the plus hides, although the row's control keeps focus (U14). In the Files view, the pointer over a modified file scrolled far right fills the whole visible row, and the pinned letter's mark backing fills with it, so the name scrolled under the letter stays hidden. The reader presses Tab: the next row's wrapper draws the row outline (a 2 px `--sidebar-ring` outline inside its edge, version 9) on its four sides, its fill unchanged, and the plus shows on that row. Tab again moves focus to the plus: the row's outline goes, since the plus is not a row control, and the plus draws its action outline, 2 px of `--sidebar-ring` outside its square; it stays shown, because something in the row still matches `:focus-visible` ([§11.7](#117-reveals-on-keyboard-focus-only)). The reader double-clicks a Thread's name: the field opens with its underline and the row draws the same ring until Enter saves the name.

**Opening a terminal from the tree (phase 5).** The reader clicks the terminal row "npm run dev" under Thread T. The terminal bundle navigates to T and opens the tab `terminal:<id>`; `layout.tabs/v1` notifies, the route names T and `current()` answers that tab, and the row takes the selected fill beside T's row. The reader selects the conversation tab: `current()` answers another id and the terminal row returns to rest.

## Contracts and versions

Versions below assume the phases land in order 1–4, then the follow-up of [§9.5](#95-follow-up-pane-rows-and-headers), written §9.5 in the Phase column, then phase 5 ([§11](#11-tree-row-states)). A phase that lands out of order bumps each bundle it touches from the version on `main` by the level stated in the "Why" column.

| Contract or bundle  | Phase | Before | After   | Why                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------- | ----- | ------ | ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |

| `terminal` bundle   | 1     | 1.1.0  | 1.1.1   | Row markup only.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `git` bundle        | 1     | 1.2.0  | 1.2.1   | Row markup only.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `layout.sidebar/v1` | 2     | 1.0.0  | 1.1.0   | New optional input method `busy` (`versioning.md:48`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `layout` bundle     | 2     | 2.0.1  | 2.1.0   | A provided contract gains a minor (`versioning.md:57`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `terminal` bundle   | 2     | 1.1.1  | 1.1.2   | Registers `busy`; no provided contract changes. `requires` keeps `layout.sidebar/v1@^1.0.0`.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

Bundles keep `engines.doric` at `^1.0.0`: they adapt to an older or newer app (`docs/features/ui-redesign.md:151`, UR25). |

Version 9: the phase 5 amendment adds no row to this table. It lands in the same pull request as phase 5, before any release of the versions above, and changes, in those versions, only class strings in markup, their doc comments, two Storybook files, the screen and mcp sheets and the tests that pin them ([§11.5](#115-where-the-classes-live-and-mixed-versions), "Versions, version 9"); no contract, export or `engines.doric` range changes.

The runtime and its contracts do not change.

## Persisted data

None new. The stack's open state stays in memory.

## Alternatives considered

- **A host field `lastExecutionStartedAt` (versions 1 and 2).** Rejected in version 3: the record already yields the start as `result.at − lastExecutionMs`. The cases it does not cover ([§1](#1-the-badge-start)) show no time rather than a wrong one, and recover at the next start. The field would have cost two runtime contract minors, a runtime release, a parity-test change and a README paragraph.
- **A host-side "working subtree" flag on each Thread.** Rejected: the host would write and publish every ancestor on each child or terminal change, and the renderer already holds every Thread of the selected Project and its terminals.
- **Counting a parent's badge from its earliest active descendant.** Rejected by U9.
- **Excluding background terminals with no timeout, or capping a badge at some age.** Rejected by D-A.
- **Resolving the Project inside `busy` with a cross-contract call on every call, as `count` does today.** Rejected by the cost requirement of [§3](#3-the-busy-answer-layoutsidebarv1-110): one call with validation and a linear lookup per Thread per render.
- **Calling `count` for every Thread to force the Project follow.** Rejected: `count` decides whether a row is expandable (`thread-branches.tsx:100`); `busy` carries its own follow.
- **A separate contract for activity (for example `layout.activity/v1`).** Rejected: one optional method on the existing registration reuses its subscription and is a minor.
- **Keeping the Threads pane as a headless, non-collapsible registration.** Rejected: `panes()` would keep listing a group the column no longer draws, and the view would keep its special cases.
- **`layout.stack/v1` 1.0.1 with the size fields ignored (versions 1–3).** Rejected by D-B: other semantics are a major (`versioning.md:49`).
- **Keeping `layout.stack/v1` as a deprecated alias over v2's store (version 4).** Rejected by the user (2026-11-10): v1's resizing could not be kept, so the alias would still change what v1 does, and every first-party consumer moves to v2 in the same phase.
- **A measured cap (ResizeObserver) for the 40% height.** Rejected: `max-height` in percent of the definite column does it in CSS.
- **Renaming `ThreadKindIcon` to `ThreadIcon` with an icon-name input (versions 1 and 2).** Rejected in version 3: an `untitled` input on the existing component does it without a rename.
- **A horizontal scrollbar on every `ScrollArea` (version 2).** Rejected in version 3: the conversation's viewport does not hide horizontal overflow (`bundles/desktop/chat/src/client/styles.css:108-110`), so wide content would gain a visible bar; the branch picker (`bundles/features/git/src/client/cwd/branch-picker.tsx:67`) and the binary-file notice (`bundles/features/files/src/client/file/file-view.tsx:147`) would also change without a requirement. The prop limits it to the two trees.
- **The mark inside the button, sticky there.** Rejected: the button clips (`overflow-hidden`, `sidebar.tsx:497`), which would make it the sticky element's scroll container.
- **A fixed right padding sized for the widest mark.** Rejected: line totals have no fixed width.
- **A zero-width sticky anchor at the row's end holding an absolutely positioned mark, with a hidden copy of the mark in the button to reserve its width (version 4).** Rejected by the user (2026-11-10): it let the background run under the mark, at the cost of an extra element per row and a duplicated mark; the in-flow sticky mark is simpler, and the background ending at the mark is accepted. Version 8 runs the fill under the mark without it: the fill moves to the row wrapper, which already holds the in-flow mark ([§11](#11-tree-row-states)).
- **Removing the tree's 4 px padding, or adding `scroll-padding`, to steer focus scrolling.** Not needed: Chromium does not scroll a partially visible element horizontally on focus ([§10.3](#103-pinned-trailing-marks)).
- **An exported `TreeRowMark` component.** Rejected: an import an older platform lacks fails the bundle; the mark is written once in each of the two files.
- **Truncating names and showing the whole path in the tooltip only.** Rejected by U10.
- **Removing `overflow-hidden` from `treeClassName`.** Rejected: the Thread tree shares it and is not part of U10.
- **Keeping `pr-8` at rest on Thread rows with the plus.** Rejected by U5; the cost is a re-truncation on hover ([SR8](#risks)).
- **Redefining what `rowInteraction(selected)` returns, with the same signature, to the button's no-fill classes (version 8).** Rejected: the platform serves `rowInteraction` to every bundle at run time, so a layout 3.0.1 on an app with phase 5 would get a button with no fill inside a wrapper that draws none, and its selected Thread row would show no selection at all; git 1.2.2's repository row would lose its hover the same way. Keeping the value and writing the new classes where each row is drawn leaves every mix of versions with visible selection ([§11.5](#115-where-the-classes-live-and-mixed-versions)).
- **Exported helpers in `desktop/ui/panels` (for example `treeRowFill(selected)` and `treeRowMarkFill(selected)`) with `PLATFORM_VERSION` 1.7.0 (version 8).** Rejected: layout, git and terminal import `desktop/ui/panels` statically (`thread-branches.tsx:1-6`, `changes-tree.tsx:8-14`, `terminal-rows.tsx:11`); a named export the app lacks fails the importing part when its module graph links (`docs/designs/ui-redesign.md:197`), so all three would raise `engines.doric` to `^1.7.0` and an older app would refuse them (`engines_unsatisfied`, `packages/bundle/src/runtime/catalog.ts:279-285`) for a visual change. §10.3's mark set the precedent of writing such markup in each file.
- **Alpha fills (`bg-sidebar-accent/50`) for hover and press (version 8).** Rejected: a translucent mark backing over a translucent row composites twice (accent at 50% over a row already at 50% reads as 75%), so the mark backing would not match the row; the user's decision takes solid mixes so that it matches exactly. Version 9 takes translucent fills again (the paper tokens, U16) and answers the same problem the other way: the row stays translucent and the mark backing alone is opaque, the ghost layered over `--sidebar` ([§11.2](#112-mark-backing)).
- **An outer focus ring on the wrapper (version 8).** Rejected: nested Thread levels clip their content (`treeClassName`'s `overflow-hidden`, `tree-guides.tsx:9-10`), so an outer ring would lose its left and right sides there; the inset ring stays inside the wrapper.
- **Removing `overflow-hidden` from `treeClassName` for the ring (version 8).** Not needed with an inset ring, and rejected because the Thread tree's levels share `treeClassName`, whose clipping they rely on (the alternative "Removing `overflow-hidden` from `treeClassName`" above).
- **Leaving terminal rows as they are (version 8).** Rejected (D23): they would keep a full-accent hover while the Thread rows beside them take the 50% mix, and keyboard focus would draw the outer ring the nested level clips.
- **Tracking the open terminal in the terminal store (version 8, SQ4).** Rejected: the tabs belong to the layout; the terminal bundle would see its own `open` calls but not the reader closing the tab or switching to another, so the row would stay selected after its tab lost selection. `layout.tabs/v1.current()` and `subscribe` answer exactly that, inside the range the bundle already requires, as the Files tree uses them.
- **Keeping the pane rows' focus fill (`group-focus-within/menu-item:bg-sidebar-accent`) (version 8, SQ3).** Rejected: it fills the row on mouse focus too, so a clicked row stays filled after the pointer leaves, and it would be a third focus look beside the row outline of every other row.
- **Relying on the app's renderer sheet for the screen and mcp classes (version 8, SQ3).** Rejected: a newer screen or mcp on an older app would lose every keyboard focus mark ([§11.5](#115-where-the-classes-live-and-mixed-versions)); a bundle sheet ships the classes with the bundle.
- **A full utility sheet for screen and mcp, with the files bundle's `@source` set (version 8, before U15).** Rejected by the user (U15): about 160 KB per bundle for a few new classes. The minimal sheet carries the cascade constraint of [§11.6](#116-pane-rows-and-the-draft-row) instead.
- **`@source` lines for the screen and mcp client sources in the files, git and terminal sheets (version 8, stage 3).** The route the layout's classes already take ([§11.5](#115-where-the-classes-live-and-mixed-versions)); it keeps every sheet the whole sorted set, so it needs no cascade check. Put to the user on 2026-11-11 and rejected by the user, who kept the minimal per-bundle sheets (U15). It would also tie the screen and mcp classes on an older app to three other bundles being active, as SR16 describes for the layout.
- **Minimal sheets that scan their bundle's `src/client/` (`@source './'`, version 8 as first written under U15).** Rejected after the cascade check (stage 3): the mcp sheet re-emits `border`, `transition-transform` and `hover:text-foreground` after rules that win today on other bundles' elements, and the two sheets leave 101 pairs to judge on every change ([§11.6](#116-pane-rows-and-the-draft-row)). The user kept the lists (SQ9, closed).
- **The scan with conflicting classes excluded (`@source not inline(…)`) (version 8, stage 3).** Rejected: the exclusion list depends on the classes of every other bundle in the window, so a change anywhere can require a new exclusion, and the 101 pairs still need judging on every change; the list of phase 5 classes has 14 or 15 tokens and no conflict.
- **The minimal sheet's utilities in a sublayer (`layer(utilities.screen)`) (version 8, stage 3).** Rules placed directly in `utilities` outrank every rule in its sublayers, so a re-emitted class could no longer override anything. Rejected: on an app built before phase 5 the keyboard reveal `group-has-[:focus-visible]/menu-item:opacity-100`, present only in the bundle sheet, would lose to `opacity-0` from the renderer sheet (`screen/src/client/elements.tsx:138`), so Remove and Retry would never show on keyboard focus.
- **The fill on the button through `group-hover/tree-row:` and `group-active/tree-row:` on rows without a mark (version 8, stage 2 review).** The button would react while the pointer is over the plus or Stop, which `group-hover` on the wrapper covers. Rejected: a Files column row still needs the fill on the wrapper to run under the mark (U13), so the trees would use two mechanisms; the ring would sit on the button, which ends where the mark begins and, on a Thread row, under the plus; and the button keeps `overflow-hidden` with its own radius (`sidebar.tsx:497`, `:701`), so the fill would not reach the wrapper's edges where the guides and the trailing slot sit. One mechanism, the wrapper, serves every row.
- **Keeping reveals on any focus (`group-focus-within`) (version 8).** Rejected by the user (U14): a row clicked with the mouse keeps its actions shown after the pointer leaves.
- **Selecting a terminal row from `current()` alone (version 8, SQ4).** Rejected: when `opener` cannot resolve the terminal's Thread it opens the tab under the route's owner (`bundles/features/terminal/src/client/index.ts:129-135`), so the row would show selected under a Thread that is not on screen; the route check of [§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions) prevents it.
- **The solid sidebar-accent design: `color-mix` fills at 50% and 80% of `--sidebar-accent` into `--sidebar`, a mark backing in the same mixes, and the `ring-2 ring-inset ring-sidebar-ring` row outline (version 8, U13, D20, D21; built at `e63bbccb`).** Superseded in version 9 by the user's decision on the preview's `theme` variant (U16), which compared it with the paper tokens on the same rows. Reasons: the project list's rows hover in `--paper-ghost-hover` (`project-item.tsx:74`) and the Dashboard and the tab strip write their focus mark as 2 px outlines (`project-item.tsx:83-84`, `tab-strip.tsx:27`), while the sidebar rows drew mixes of the vendored sidebar's `--sidebar-accent` and a `ring-2` box shadow, a different token family and a different mark; and a box shadow is not drawn in forced-colors mode, while an outline is (CSS Color Adjustment Level 1, §3.1) ([§11](#11-tree-row-states) lists what version 9 copies and what it adds). Its structure (whole-row fill on the wrapper, opaque mark backing, inset focus mark, selectors, keyboard reveals) is kept; only the colors and the focus mark's drawing change.
- **Opaque mixes equal to the ghost composite (for example `color-mix(in srgb, var(--sidebar-foreground) 5.9%, var(--sidebar))`) on the rows and the backing (version 9).** Rejected: the user chose the tokens themselves; a mix would copy their alpha into the class and drift from `--paper-ghost-hover` and `--paper-ghost-pressed` if `tokens.ts` changes their values. The gradient backing reads the same tokens.
- **A translucent mark backing, `group-hover/tree-row:bg-[var(--paper-ghost-hover)]` (version 9).** Rejected: the backing would let a scrolled name show through and would composite the ghost twice over the row's own ghost ([§11.2](#112-mark-backing)).
- **The `background` shorthand for the backing, `group-hover/tree-row:[background:linear-gradient(G,G),var(--sidebar)]` and the same under `group-active` (the first draft of version 9).** Rejected on the scope review: it repeats `--sidebar`, which `bg-sidebar` already sets, and the shorthand resets every other background longhand of the element; an image layer over `bg-sidebar`'s color gives the same color with one property ([§11.2](#112-mark-backing)). `bg-[linear-gradient(G,G),var(--sidebar)]` is not an option either: Tailwind writes it to `background-image`, where a color is invalid.
- **Keeping the component hovers of Remove, Retry and Stop (version 9).** Rejected (D29): on the ghost fills their `--sidebar-accent` squares differ from a hovered row by 1.04:1, lighter than the row in the light theme and darker in the dark theme, the direction opposite to the row's own hover ([§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions)).
- **The actions' own focus rings alone: `focus-visible:ring-2` in `--sidebar-ring`, and `Button`'s `focus-visible:ring-3` in `--ring` at 50% on Stop (version 9, first draft).** Rejected by the user (U17): the actions take the rows' outline too. The rings of the plus, Remove, Retry and Sign in stay under the outline (D29).
- **`focus-visible:ring-0` on every action, so that the outline is the only mark (version 9, after the scope review).** Rejected on the stage 2 review (Critique M1, the user's decision of 2026-11-11): older renderer sheets hold `focus-visible:ring-0` but not `focus-visible:outline-solid` or `focus-visible:outline-[var(--sidebar-ring)]`, so in SR16's window the plus would lose its ring and draw no outline ([§11.5](#115-where-the-classes-live-and-mixed-versions), "Mixed versions"). Stop keeps it: its 3 px ring would show beyond the outline, and its classes always come with the terminal sheet ([§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions)).
- **The outline in `--paper-focus`, as the preview's `theme` variant drew it (version 9, SQ10 (a)).** Rejected by the user (SQ10, answer (b)): in the light theme it has 2.36:1 against `--sidebar` and less against every row fill ([Risks](#risks), SR19).
- **The outline in `--paper-blue-3`, or a new light value of `--paper-focus` (SQ10 (c), (d)).** Rejected by the user (SQ10, answer (b)). `--paper-blue-3` is darker than the Dashboard's focus mark; changing `--paper-focus` in `packages/desktop/src/tokens/tokens.ts:115` would change the Dashboard and the tab strip as well.
- **An inset outline on the row actions, the rows' form (version 9).** Rejected: its neighbors are the same as the outer outline's, and on the 20 px actions it would cover the square's edge up to the 16 px icon; the outer form keeps the version 8 ring's position ([§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions)).
- **`--paper-thumbnail-selected` as the selected fill, so that the selected fill differs from `--sidebar` more than the hover fill (SQ11 (b)).** Rejected by the user (SQ11, kept). In the light theme it has 1.04:1 against `--sidebar`, weaker than both the current selected fill (1.07:1) and the hover fill (1.12:1), so it would not reach the goal there; only the dark theme (1.30:1) would ([§11](#11-tree-row-states)).
- **Changing the hover and focus defaults in `sidebar.tsx` and `button.tsx` (version 9).** Rejected: both are vendored with recorded patches and served to every bundle at run time; the `ghost` variant has 64 call sites, and phase 5 wrote every row class at its call site ([§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions)).

## Risks

- **SR1 — The 40% cap.** If the column's height were not definite, `max-h-[40%]` would not apply and an open pane would grow to its content, leaving the tree no height. Mitigation: [§9.3](#93-stack-panes) traces the chain to `h-svh`; happy-dom computes no layout, so the [1:1 verification](#11-verification) measures it.
- **SR2 — Short windows.** Let H be the column's height. The search block takes 48 px (36 + 12), the bottom padding 14 px, the Thread region's button and gap 40 px. With both panes open at their cap (0.4·H each), the tree's height is H − 48 − 0.8·H − 40 − 14 = 0.2·H − 102 px: zero at H = 510 px, one 28 px row at H = 650 px, about 105 px (3 rows) at the captured H = 1038 px. With one pane open at its cap and the other closed (28 px), it is 0.6·H − 130 px. Below 158 px (48 + 40 + two 28 px headers + 14) the column overflows and `Sidebar` clips its bottom. Accepted: U4 fixed the cap; the reader collapses a pane.
- **SR3 — Cost of `busy`.** `busy` is called for every Thread on every render, and resolving a Thread's Project crosses a contract. Mitigation: the bound of [§3](#3-the-busy-answer-layoutsidebarv1-110) (no repeated cross-contract call per Thread; linear time per render), which the plan meets and the review of the implementation checks. Measurement trigger: if a sidebar render with 500 Threads and 100 terminals takes more than 16 ms in a profile, the mechanism is revisited.
- **SR4 — Side effect during render.** `busy`, like `count` today, may start a Project follow during the layout's render. The subscription is asynchronous, so no store updates synchronously inside render.
- **SR5 — No time in edge cases.** An idle active Thread without a badge start ([§1](#1-the-badge-start)) shows the shimmer and no time until its next start. The hidden status keeps "Agent running" for assistive technology. Accepted: no wrong time is shown.
- **SR6 — Mixed sidebar versions.** A terminal bundle on a `layout.sidebar/v1` 1.0.0 gets no `busy` calls; a layout 2.1.0 with a terminal bundle without `busy` gets no answers. In both, rows are active only through running Threads, never through agent terminals; nothing else changes.
- **SR7 — Long-lived activity.** An agent background terminal with no timeout keeps its Thread and ancestors active until it exits, for days if it runs that long (D-A). The badge stays within 8 characters (D6).
- **SR8 — Hover re-truncation.** A long Thread name on a row with the plus re-truncates while the row is hovered or focused. Accepted (U5).
- **SR9 — Lost open-state cue on hover.** Without the Hover swap, an expandable row shows no open or closed cue on hover; the visible children under an open row remain the cue, and every expandable row of the three trees exposes `aria-expanded`. Accepted (U11).
- **SR10 — Long names widen the tree.** One very long name makes every row of that Files column tree as wide; the reader scrolls to read it. Accepted (U10).
- **SR11 — Sticky depends on unclipped ancestors.** Any `overflow` other than `visible` added later between the mark and the viewport stops the pinning. Mitigation: T-4.1 asserts the `FileTree` wrapper and directory level, T-4.4 the repository row's wrapper and group, and T-4.5 and T-4.6 the two trees and viewports.
- **SR12 — Focus scrolling.** The no-horizontal-scroll behavior rests on Chromium's focus rule ([§10.3](#103-pinned-trailing-marks)). A future Chromium that scrolled partially visible elements would move `scrollLeft` by up to 4 px on Tab. Mitigation: the 1:1 verification checks it on each Electron upgrade that touches this view.
- **SR13 — Security.** No new data crosses a boundary. `busy` runs in the renderer with a Thread id, and a throw is contained. No new permission, IPC or storage path.
- **SR14 — Mixed platform and bundle versions in the Files column.** A new `files` or `git` on an older platform: the tree is as wide as its widest row, but the old `ScrollArea` draws no horizontal scrollbar. A trackpad or Shift+wheel scrolls sideways, because the viewport scrolls on both axes (`node_modules/@base-ui/react/scroll-area/viewport/ScrollAreaViewport.mjs:279`); a mouse-only reader without Shift+wheel cannot. The old `FileTree` keeps its clipped rows with marks at their far end. An old host on the new platform keeps `overflow-x-hidden!` and a tree as wide as the viewport: rows look and behave as today.
- **SR15 — Removed `layout.stack/v1`.** On a layout 3.0.0, any bundle that still requires `layout.stack/v1` is quarantined with the unmet range as its reason, and its pane does not show; in this repository none remains after phase 3, and the planned Ports pane's document moves to v2. A new Screens or MCP on a layout older than 3.0.0 is quarantined the same way ([§9.4](#94-layoutstackv2)). Accepted (user decision, 2026-11-10).
- **SR16 — Mixed app and bundle versions in the tree rows (version 8).** A newer layout, git or terminal on an app built before phase 5 draws the new Thread, terminal and repository rows while the app's `FileTree` keeps today's: within the Changes view, a repository row fills on hover and its files do not. The layout's new classes then come from the git 1.2.3 and terminal 1.1.3 sheets, and from the files sheet only when the files bundle, which keeps 1.7.1, was built from a tree that includes phase 5 ([§11.5](#115-where-the-classes-live-and-mixed-versions)); if neither git nor terminal were active in the window, the older renderer sheet would lack them and Thread rows would draw neither the hover fill nor the row outline, while `bg-sidebar-accent` (already in that sheet) still marks the selected row. Older bundles on a newer app draw today's rows and the new `FileTree`. Nothing fails in either direction and selection stays visible. The layout's path depends on the `@source` lines of the layout client in the files, git and terminal sheets (`bundles/features/files/src/client/styles.css:18`, `bundles/features/git/src/client/styles.css:21`, `bundles/features/terminal/src/client/styles.css:33`); a later change that drops them would remove the layout's new classes on an older app. That dependency predates phase 5: every layout class written after an app was built has reached that app this way since WP-10N (`docs/architecture/bundles/implementation-decisions.md:991`), and no test guards it. Phase 5 adds no guard either: a test that the three sheets still scan the layout client belongs with the sheets' own rule, outside this spec; until one exists, the review of a change to those `@source` lines checks it. Version 9: in the same window the plus shows its version 8 ring under Tab and no hover square, unless screen 2.0.3 or mcp 1.2.4 is active ([§11.5](#115-where-the-classes-live-and-mixed-versions), "Mixed versions"); D29 keeps the actions' rings for this case. Accepted by this spec: the mismatch ends when the window runs an app and bundles that all include phase 5.
- **SR17 — Copies of the class strings across bundles (version 8; version 9 adds the action sets).** No set crosses a bundle boundary ([§11.5](#115-where-the-classes-live-and-mixed-versions)), so each of the six shipping units that draws rows holds its own copy of the sets it uses: the platform (`file-tree.tsx`), layout (`thread-branches.tsx` and `draft-name.tsx`), git (`changes-tree.tsx`), terminal (`terminal-rows.tsx`), screen and mcp (each `elements.tsx`). That makes six shipped copies when each unit shares its sets among its files, and seven when layout keeps one in each of its two files; the plan decides. Two more are stories: `bundles/desktop/layout/stories/support/terminal-rows.tsx` and `packages/desktop/stories/panels/tree-guides.stories.tsx`, which may share their unit's copy or write their own. The element table of §11.5 states which sets each file uses. Copies can drift. Mitigation for the shipped copies: T-5.1–T-5.6, T-5.9–T-5.11, T-5.13 and T-5.14 assert the token lists of [§11](#11-tree-row-states) in each. Those tests compare each copy with the spec's lists, not the copies with one another, so a change made to one copy and to its test without the others passes; the review of such a change checks the other copies against §11, and the Files to change of a later spec list every copy. The two story copies are accepted to drift: no test renders them, they do not ship, and the [1:1 verification](#11-verification) runs in the app, not in the stories. Phase 5 writes them from the same table, and a later change to a set that leaves a story behind shows only in Storybook. Version 9 adds the action sets, written at six call sites of four bundles and in the story's Stop ([§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions)); the T-5 tests of those bundles assert them ([Phase 5 amendment tests](#phase-5-amendment-tests-version-9)), and [§11](#11-tree-row-states) ("What a token choice costs") counts the places a token is written.
- **SR18 — Pressed fill from a row action (version 8; version 9 colors).** The wrapper's `:active` also matches while the plus, Stop, a pane row's Remove or Retry, or MCP's Sign in is pressed, so the row shows its pressed fill under the action (the 80% mix in version 8, `--paper-ghost-pressed` in version 9). Accepted by this spec: the action belongs to the row.
- **SR19 — Low-contrast fills, the focus outline's contrast and guide crossings (version 8; redone in version 9).** Computed on 2026-11-11 from `tokens.css` (`:31`, `:35`, `:38`, `:49-50`; dark `:126`, `:130`, `:133`, `:144-145`) with sRGB alpha compositing and WCAG 2.2 relative luminance.
  - Fills. Version 9's hover fill over `--sidebar` is `#e7e2da` on `#f3eee5` in the light theme, 1.12:1, and `#302c28` on `#24211d` in the dark theme, 1.16:1; the pressed fill is 1.17:1 and 1.26:1. Hover and press are pointer feedback, not information, and keyboard focus is shown by the outline, so WCAG 2.2 success criterion 1.4.11 (Non-text Contrast, 3:1 for the visual information that identifies a state) applies to the outline, not to these fills. The selected fill identifies a state: SR23. The user chose the tokens on the preview (U16).
  - Focus outlines. The row outline is 2 px of `--sidebar-ring` inside the row's edge; the actions' outline is 2 px of `--sidebar-ring` just outside the action (U17, SQ10 (b)). Each one's adjacent colors are a row fill or `--sidebar`, and, for an action under the pointer, its hover square. Ratios of `--sidebar-ring` against each:

    | Theme, `--sidebar-ring` | `--sidebar` (rest) | `--sidebar-accent` (selected) | hover fill | pressed fill | action square on a hovered, selected or pressed row |
    | ----------------------- | ------------------ | ----------------------------- | ---------- | ------------ | --------------------------------------------------- |
    | Light, `#8a7f70`        | 3.40:1             | 3.16:1                        | 3.04:1     | 2.90:1       | 2.61:1, 2.71:1, 2.50:1                              |
    | Dark, `#d9cdb6`         | 10.19:1            | 9.19:1                        | 8.81:1     | 8.12:1       | 6.96:1, 7.29:1, 6.46:1                              |

    The dark theme passes WCAG 2.2 1.4.11's 3:1 against every color. In the light theme, a row or action focused from the keyboard with no pointer on it lies on `--sidebar` or `--sidebar-accent`, at 3.40:1 or 3.16:1, and passes. It drops below 3:1 only while the pointer is also on the focused element: a focused row held pressed (2.90:1), or a focused action under the pointer, whose own square lies inside the outline (2.50:1 to 2.71:1), while its outer side stays on the row fill (2.90:1 to 3.40:1). Accepted by this spec: the drop lasts only while the pointer is on the focused element, and that element then also shows the pointer's own mark, the row's pressed fill or the action's hover square. The rejected `--paper-focus` failed at rest as well ([Alternatives considered](#alternatives-considered), SQ10).

  - Guides. The 1 px guide lines (`z-10`) paint over the outline's top and bottom edges where they cross them, as over the version 8 ring. Accepted by the user on the preview (U13).

- **SR20 — Two minimal bundle sheets (version 8, U15; lists rewritten in version 9).** The screen and mcp sheets depart from the repository's rule that every utility sheet emits the whole sorted set ([§11.6](#116-pane-rows-and-the-draft-row)): they re-emit 18 and 19 classes after the renderer sheet, in every window. A re-emitted class R overrides a rule Q of an earlier sheet on an element that matches both, when Q sets one of R's properties (or a longhand of it), has R's specificity, computed from the compiled selectors, and sorts after R: a variant that adds nothing (a media, container or `supports-*` variant; a named shadcn data variant such as `data-open:` or `data-active:`; `in-*`, `ltr:` or `rtl:`), a child or descendant rule on the parent (`*:w-full`, `**:…`), a longhand after a shorthand (`border-l-3` after `border`), or a variant of equal weight sorted later (`dark:` after `hover:`; `data-open:hover:` after `hover:`; a bracketed `data-[…]:` or `aria-*:`, which add an attribute selector, after a pseudo-class variant). Mitigation: the sheets list only the phase 5 classes, not their bundles' scanned source, which the check found in conflict (comment lines, the thread rail under reduced motion, dark tabs); the cascade check of §11.6 finds no conflict for the version 8 lists at `7e0237ba` and for the version 9 lists at `e63bbccb`; T-5.12 pins each built sheet to its list, so a change to a list fails until the check is rerun. Remaining risks: a class added later elsewhere in the window (another bundle, a platform component) can form a new pair with a listed class, and no test catches it; the review of a change that adds, anywhere in the window, a class with a variant over a property the lists set (background color, box shadow, outline style, width, offset and color, opacity, pointer events, `right`, border radius) runs the check. A class added later to screen or mcp outside the lists reaches an app built before it only through that app's renderer sheet, as every screen and mcp class did before phase 5. The sheets add about 4.8 KB each.
- **SR21 — Row outline clipped on wide Files rows (version 8).** When a Files column tree is wider than its viewport, a focused row's outline shows only its top, bottom and the side inside the viewport ([§11.3](#113-keyboard-focus-and-clipping)). No mitigation in phase 5; the fill and the visible edges still mark the row. Accepted by this spec, the default of SQ6, which the user did not answer.
- **SR22 — Terminal selection on every tab change (version 8).** The layout mounts a terminal `Rows` element under every expanded Thread (`thread-branches.tsx:211-221`); those whose Thread has terminals each hold one `layout.tabs/v1` subscription, and those whose Thread has none hold none (§11.4, data flow). Each subscribed element reads the route on each notification, which also fires on every workspace tree change (§11.4, Cost): E route reads and one O(T) tab read per notification, and at most two re-renders. Measurement, run once in phase 5's [1:1 verification](#11-verification) and on any report of slow tab switching: a production build of the app, one Project with 50 root Threads, each expanded and each with one open terminal, and 10 tabs on the active owner; in the developer tools' Performance panel, record 20 clicks that each select a different tab; for each click, sum the time of the terminal bundle's `layout.tabs/v1` listeners and of the `Rows` renders they cause (their call trees under the notification's task). Trigger: if the mean over the 20 clicks is above 2 ms, the subscription moves to one shared store per window that computes the selected terminal once and hands it to every `Rows`. The pull request of phase 5 records the measured mean.
- **SR23 — The selected fill's contrast (version 8; measured in version 9).** The selected fill `bg-sidebar-accent` is the only visual mark of a selected Thread, `FileTree` file or terminal row: the selected button's `data-active:font-normal` keeps the default weight and its `data-active:text-sidebar-accent-foreground` is equal to the rest color `--sidebar-foreground` (`sidebar.tsx:497`, `:701`; `tokens.css:32`, `:36`, `:127`, `:131`). Against `--sidebar` the fill has 1.07:1 in the light theme and 1.11:1 in the dark theme, below WCAG 2.2 1.4.11's 3:1 for visual information that identifies a state; the hover fill, 1.12:1 and 1.16:1, differs from `--sidebar` more ([§11](#11-tree-row-states)). This predates phase 5: the selected button drew the same `bg-sidebar-accent` at `bb4c2453` (`data-active:bg-sidebar-accent`, `sidebar.tsx:497`). Other cues, at `e63bbccb`: the selected `FileTree` row's treeitem has `aria-selected="true"` (`packages/desktop/src/panels/file-tree.tsx:288`) and the selected terminal row's button `aria-current="true"` ([§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions)); a selected Thread row's treeitem had neither at `e63bbccb` (`thread-branches.tsx:162-177`); since the follow-up of 2026-11-11, Thread and sub-thread treeitems carry `aria-selected={selected}`, as `FileTree` does, and only the selected main Thread's is `"true"` (selecting a sub-thread selects its main Thread and opens the child as a tab; T-5.18, T-5.19). On screen, the workspace shows the selected Thread's tabs, with the selected file's or terminal's tab marked in the tab strip (`packages/desktop/src/panels/tab-strip.tsx:180`; Terms, "Tab, tab owner, selected tab"). The `ThreadBreadcrumb` component (`bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/molecules/thread-breadcrumb.tsx`) is mounted only by its stories and `bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/shell.test.ts:1598`, not by the app, so it is no cue. Accepted by the user, who kept the selected fill (SQ11, U17). Mitigated in part by the follow-up of 2026-11-11 (`aria-selected` on Thread rows); a second visual mark would be a later change.

## Phases

Each phase leaves `nx run-many -t lint typecheck test` green for the projects it touches, and updates the manifest tests for the versions it moves.

1. **Shared visuals.** [§5](#5-the-untitled-icon)–[§8](#8-terminal-lead-slot): Untitled icon, no Hover swap and `aria-expanded` on Thread rows, 20 px steps, terminal lead slot.
2. **Activity and rows.** [§1](#1-the-badge-start)–[§4](#4-thread-rows): badge start, activity rule, `layout.sidebar/v1` 1.1.0 and the terminal's `busy`, elapsed text, repository marks removed.
3. **Column.** [§9](#9-the-sidebar-column): Threads pane removed, New Thread under the tree, panes pinned without resizing, `layout.stack/v2`, Screens and MCP migrated.
4. **Files column scrolling.** [§10](#10-files-column-horizontal-scrolling): `ScrollArea` prop and `PLATFORM_VERSION` 1.6.0, `FileTree` rows and their sticky marks, the two hosts and the repository row. It depends on no other phase.

Follow-up ([§9.5](#95-follow-up-pane-rows-and-headers)): pane rows, empty notices and the header reveal, after phase 3.

5. **Tree row states** (version 8, `accepted`; implemented 2026-11-11, PR #57). [§11](#11-tree-row-states): the row fill on the wrapper, the opaque mark backing, the row outline, terminal rows and their selected state, the Screens and MCP pane rows with their minimal bundle sheets, the draft row, the row outline on rows being renamed, keyboard-only reveals, and layout 3.0.2, git 1.2.3, terminal 1.1.3, screen 2.0.3, mcp 1.2.4. It comes after phases 1, 2 and 4, whose row markup (lead slots, `threadRowPadding`, the in-flow mark) it edits, and after §9.5, whose layout version it moves.

6. **Phase 5 amendment** (version 9, `accepted`; implemented 2026-11-11, PR #57). The paper-token restyle of [§11](#11-tree-row-states) (U16, U17, D27–D29), one change on top of `e63bbccb` in the same pull request. It rewrites the class sets where phase 5 wrote them ([§11.5](#115-where-the-classes-live-and-mixed-versions)), adds the action hover, Stop hover, action outline and Stop ring off sets, rewrites the screen and mcp lists ([§11.6](#116-pane-rows-and-the-draft-row)) and the tests that pin the class strings ([Test plan](#test-plan)), and moves no version. It leaves `nx run-many -t lint typecheck test` green for the projects it touches: `desktop` (`packages/desktop`), `bundle-layout`, `bundle-git`, `bundle-terminal`, `bundle-screen` and `bundle-mcp` (their `project.json` names).

### Phase 5 amendment (version 9)

Lines at `e63bbccb`. This list is the one place that gives, per file, where phase 5 wrote the tokens version 9 rewrites and where the new sets go ([§11.5](#115-where-the-classes-live-and-mixed-versions) points here). Each file's doc comments that name the version 8 colors or the inset ring are reworded with its tokens.

- `packages/desktop/src/panels/file-tree.tsx`: the unselected fill, the treeitem outline and the unselected mark backing (`:30-50`).
- `bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/domain/sidebar.ts`: the unselected fill, the treeitem outline and the textbox outline (`:33-47`), which `thread-branches.tsx` and `draft-name.tsx` import and which they do not change.
- `bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/molecules/row-add-action.tsx`: the plus's `className` (`:72`) adds the action hover and action outline sets.
- `bundles/features/git/src/client/changes/changes-tree.tsx`: the unselected fill, the treeitem outline and the unselected mark backing (`:37-50`).
- `bundles/features/terminal/src/client/rows/terminal-rows.tsx`: the unselected fill, the sub-button outline and the input outline (`:39-53`); Stop's `className` (`:184`) adds the Stop hover, action outline and Stop ring off sets.
- `bundles/features/screen/src/client/elements.tsx`: the row's unselected fill and menu-button outline (`:141-142`); Retry's and Remove's `className` (`:192`, `:208`) add the action hover and action outline sets. `bundles/features/screen/src/client/styles.css:8`: the version 9 list of [§11.6](#116-pane-rows-and-the-draft-row).
- `bundles/features/mcp/src/client/elements.tsx`: the row's unselected fill and menu-button outline (`:119-120`); Remove's `className` (`:192`) adds the action hover and action outline sets; Sign in's class string (`:179`) adds the action outline set and keeps the rest, `ring-sidebar-ring` and `focus-visible:ring-2` included. `bundles/features/mcp/src/client/styles.css:8`: the version 9 list.
- Stories: `bundles/desktop/layout/stories/support/terminal-rows.tsx` (the item's ring, `:36`; Stop's Stop hover, action outline and Stop ring off sets, `:66`) and `packages/desktop/stories/panels/tree-guides.stories.tsx` (the `Depths` fills, `:29`).
- Tests: those listed under [Phase 5 amendment tests](#phase-5-amendment-tests-version-9).
- Not changed: `packages/desktop/src/components/sidebar.tsx` and `button.tsx` ([§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions)), `packages/desktop/src/panels/tree-guides.tsx` (`rowInteraction`, deprecated and frozen), `packages/desktop/src/tokens/tokens.ts` and `packages/desktop/src/styles/tokens.css` (the tokens are used as they are, SQ10), `draft-name.tsx` (it reads layout's shared sets), every manifest and `packages/bundle/src/runtime/version.ts` (no version moves), the files, git and terminal sheets (they scan the sources that change). `thread-branches.tsx`, `project-item.tsx` and `packages/desktop/src/comment-pin.tsx` change in the follow-up below.

### Follow-up of 2026-11-11

- `bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/molecules/thread-branches.tsx`: Thread and sub-thread treeitems carry `aria-selected={selected}` (`:168`); only the selected main Thread's is `"true"` (SR23).
- `bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/components/molecules/project-item.tsx`: the grid button (`:83`) and the list button (`:84`) add `focus-visible:outline-solid`, so their `outline-none` no longer hides the focus outline ([§11](#11-tree-row-states)).
- `packages/desktop/src/comment-pin.tsx`: the pin (`:27`) adds `focus-visible:outline-solid` for the same reason.
- Tests: `bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/thread-rows.test.ts` (T-5.18, T-5.19), `bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/dashboard-shell.test.ts` (T-5.20) and `packages/desktop/tests/comment-pin.test.ts` (T-5.21); see [Follow-up tests of 2026-11-11](#follow-up-tests-of-2026-11-11).

## Test plan

Tests go through public exports and rendered markup, as the existing suites do. happy-dom computes no layout, so geometry is read from the classes that draw it (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/thread-rows.test.ts:11-12`).

### Phase 1 tests

- **T-1.1** (`packages/desktop/tests/tree-guides.test.ts`) — `indentation` gives 6, 26 and 66 px at depths 0, 1 and 3; `TreeGuides` at depth 3 draws guides at 14, 34 and 54 px.
- **T-1.2** (`bundles/features/terminal/tests/client/render.test.ts`) — Every terminal row's button holds one `[data-slot="terminal-lead"]` of 16 px (`size-4`) with one `svg.lucide-terminal` in it, and no `svg` as its own direct child.
- **T-1.3** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/thread-kind.test.ts`) — `isUntitled` is true for `''` and false for any other name, including the literal `'Untitled'`. The `threadKindIcon` table test is unchanged.
- **T-1.4** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/thread-rows.test.ts`) — An Untitled row's lead slot holds `svg.lucide-message-circle-dashed` and no kind icon; a named row's holds no Untitled icon.
- **T-1.5** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/shell.test.ts`) — With an Untitled Thread selected, the breadcrumb's last crumb holds `svg.lucide-message-circle-dashed`; a conversation tab of that Thread holds it in its trigger; a tab of a Thread missing from the tree keeps `svg.lucide-message-circle`.
- **T-1.6** (U11; `bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/thread-rows.test.ts`, replacing the two Hover swap cases) — The lead slot of every Thread row, open, closed or not expandable, holds exactly one `svg`: its kind icon, with no `hidden`, `group-hover/tree-row:*` or `group-focus-within/tree-row:*` class; no tree row draws `svg.lucide-chevron-right`. Selecting an expandable row and activating it again still collapses it.
- **T-1.7** (U11; `packages/desktop/tests/panels.test.ts`, replacing the two directory swap cases) — A closed directory's lead slot holds only `svg.lucide-folder`, an open one's only `svg.lucide-folder-open`, neither with a hover or focus class; no row draws `svg.lucide-chevron-right`; directory rows keep `aria-expanded` `false` and `true`.
- **T-1.8** (U11; `bundles/features/git/tests/changes-tree.test.ts`, replacing the two repository swap cases) — The repository row's lead slot holds only `svg.lucide-git-branch` while closed and while open, with no hover or focus class, and keeps `aria-expanded`.
- **T-1.9** (SQ2; `bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/thread-rows.test.ts`) — A Thread row with a child Thread has `aria-expanded="true"` on its `[role="treeitem"]` while open and `"false"` after it is collapsed; a row whose only children are registered rows (a fixture with a terminal under the Thread) has it too; a row with no children has no `aria-expanded` attribute.
- **T-1.10** (versions) — `bundles/desktop/layout/tests/tabs-menus.test.ts` asserts layout 2.0.1; `bundles/features/terminal/tests/manifest.test.ts` terminal 1.1.1; `bundles/features/git/tests/manifest.test.ts` git 1.2.1.

### Phase 2 tests

- **T-2.1** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/thread-tree.test.ts`) — An idle tree with `busy` always false gives an empty map.
- **T-2.2** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/thread-tree.test.ts`) — A running grandchild G of child C of root R marks G, C and R; R's idle sibling has no entry. G's `startedAt` is its `executionStartedAt`. C, idle with `result.at` = t1 and `lastExecutionMs` = 90 000, has `startedAt` = t1 − 90 s.
- **T-2.3** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/thread-tree.test.ts`) — `busy` true for an idle leaf marks the leaf and every ancestor. With `busy` false everywhere, a record in `state: 'running'` without `activePromptId` marks nothing (`isThreadRunning` false).
- **T-2.4** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/thread-tree.test.ts`) — An active idle Thread with no `result`, and one with a `result` but no `lastExecutionMs` (a paused latest prompt), each get an entry with no `startedAt` (D2).
- **T-2.5** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/thread-tree.test.ts`) — `busy` is called exactly once per Thread of the level, including Threads under a node the caller draws collapsed.
- **T-2.6** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/sidebar-service.test.ts`) — `addThreadRows` accepts a registration with a `busy` function and one without, and refuses `busy: 1` with the validator's message. `threadRowsBusy` is false with no `busy`, false when `busy` throws, and true when any registration answers true; a throw is not logged.
- **T-2.7** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/thread-rows.test.ts`, rendered workspace) — With Plan idle (`result.at` one minute ago, `lastExecutionMs` four minutes) and its child Form running, Plan's row has one `[role="status"]` named "Agent running" whose text starts `05:`, and Plan's name has the class `shimmer`; Audit, idle with nothing under it, has neither. A fixture with `terminalBusy: [validate.id]` and nothing running marks Validate, Form and Plan; after collapsing Plan, Plan stays active. A Plan with no `lastExecutionMs` and a running child has the `shimmer` class and one `[role="status"]` named "Agent running" with the class `sr-only` and no text.
- **T-2.8** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/thread-rows.test.ts`, replacing the two repository-mark cases) — No row draws `span[title="Git repository"]` or `span[title="GitHub repository"]` though Threads have `cwdRepo`; the elapsed badge sits at the row's right edge (`right-1`). Each row's button carries `threadRowPadding`'s classes for its state: a timed row with the plus has `pr-14`, and its elapsed badge has `group-hover/tree-row:opacity-0` and `group-focus-within/tree-row:opacity-0` while the plus has `group-hover/tree-row:opacity-100`; an idle row with the plus has `pr-1.5` and `group-hover/tree-row:pr-8`; an active row without a badge start has `pr-1.5`; no row has `pr-16` or `pr-8` at rest.
- **T-2.9** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/duration.test.ts`) — `elapsedBadge` gives `00:00` for −5 s and 0; `05:12`; `1:00:00` at 1 h; `99:59:59` just under 100 h; `4d 4h` at 100 h; `99d 23h` just under 100 days; `99d+` at 100 days and at 400 days. Below 100 h, `elapsedBadge(ms)` equals `duration(ms)` for 0, 59 s, 1 h and 99 h 59 min 59 s. `duration` keeps its cases.
- **T-2.10** (new `bundles/features/terminal/tests/client/sidebar-rows.test.ts`) — `registerTerminalSurfaces` with fakes for the regions, the sidebar (capturing the registration), the store and `workspace().thread`: `busy` is true for a Thread whose terminal is agent and `running` or `starting`, false for agent `exited` and for user `running`; the first `busy` call follows the Thread's Project when `count` was never called; `subscribe` passes the store's listener through. With five Threads of one Project, a first round of `busy` for each makes at most five calls to the fake `workspace().thread`, and a second round makes none; a Thread for which the fake first answers `undefined` is asked again on the next call. After the store reports the agent terminal exited, `busy` answers false for its Thread; after it reports a new agent terminal starting under another Thread, `busy` answers true for that one. `count` keeps its answers.
- **T-2.11** (versions) — `bundles/desktop/layout/tests/tabs-menus.test.ts` asserts layout 2.1.0 providing `layout.sidebar/v1@1.1.0`; `bundles/features/terminal/tests/manifest.test.ts` terminal 1.1.2 requiring `layout.sidebar/v1@^1.0.0`.
- **T-2.12** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/sidebar.test.ts`) — `threadRowPadding` returns `'pr-14'` for (timed, with the plus) and (timed, without), `'pr-1.5 group-hover/tree-row:pr-8 group-focus-within/tree-row:pr-8'` for (not timed, with the plus) and `'pr-1.5'` for (not timed, without).

### Phase 3 tests

- **T-3.1** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/search.test.ts`, rewriting the Threads-header case) — With no registered pane and with the Screens pane, no button is named "Collapse Threads" or "Expand Threads"; with the Screens pane, one is named "Expand Screens".
- **T-3.2** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/shell.test.ts`, replacing "the Threads pane header has no New thread control") — The button named "New Thread" follows every `[role="treeitem"]`, has no text content, holds one `svg.lucide-plus`, and is full width with the ghost fills (`w-full`, `bg-[var(--paper-ghost-hover)]`, `hover:bg-[var(--paper-ghost-pressed)]`, and neither `bg-secondary` nor the secondary hover mix; before the follow-up of 2026-11-12, `bg-secondary`); every `[data-slot="sidebar-pane"]` follows it. Inside `[data-slot="thread-tree"]`, the error alert comes before the rows; while loading, the skeleton rows are there; with a query that matches nothing, `[data-threads-empty]` is its last child and the New Thread button follows the scroller.
- **T-3.3** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/shell.test.ts`) — The sidebar holds no `[data-slot="resizable-handle"]` and no `[data-slot="resizable-panel-group"]`.
- **T-3.4** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/shell.test.ts`) — Each `[data-slot="sidebar-pane"]` has `max-h-[40%]` and `min-h-7`; once expanded, its content has `min-h-0` and `overflow-y-auto`. `[data-slot="thread-region"]` has `flex-1` and `min-h-10`; `[data-slot="thread-tree"]` has `min-h-0` and `overflow-y-auto`; the New Thread button has `shrink-0`.
- **T-3.5** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/shell.test.ts`) — "Collapse Screens" removes the Screens content and keeps its header; "Expand Screens" brings it back. After activation, `layout.stack/v2.panes()` lists only the fixture's panes, with no `threads` id.
- **T-3.6** (new `bundles/desktop/layout/tests/stack-service.test.ts`) — Through `createTestServices({ bundle: { id: 'screen' }, provide: [createLayoutStack()] })` and `services.use(LayoutStackV2)`, as `bundles/desktop/layout/tests/sidebar-service.test.ts:39-48` does for the sidebar: `register` with `minSize`, with `maxSize` and with `defaultSize` each throws a `ContractError` with code `invalid_input`, because the strict `stackPaneSchema` (`contracts/index.ts:387-401`) runs at dispatch (`packages/bundle/src/runtime/dispatch.ts:177`); the store itself checks no schema (`stores/stack.ts:30-43`). `register` without them succeeds, and `setOpen` and `toggle` change `isOpen`.
- **T-3.7** (`bundles/features/screen/tests/client/sidebar.test.ts`, `bundles/features/mcp/tests/client/sidebar.test.ts:975-978`) — Each bundle registers its pane through `layout.stack/v2`, without `minSize` or `maxSize`.
- **T-3.8** (versions) — `bundles/desktop/layout/tests/tabs-menus.test.ts` asserts layout 3.0.1 (3.0.0 at phase 3) providing `layout.stack/v2@2.0.0` and no `layout.stack/v1`; the Screens and MCP sidebar tests read their manifests and assert screen 2.0.2 and mcp 1.2.3 (2.0.1 and 1.2.2 at phase 3), each requiring `layout.stack/v2@^2.0.0` and not `layout.stack/v1`.
- **T-3.9** (unchanged, still passing) — `bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/shell.test.ts`: New Thread opens the draft row at the end of the tree, stays disabled without a Project and clears a query; Screens sits below the tree. `bundles/desktop/layout/tests/stack-store.test.ts`: the existing cases.

### Follow-up tests (§9.5)

- **T-F.1** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/shell.test.ts`) — A pane header reveals its action only while it is hovered or holds focus.
- **T-F.2** (`bundles/features/screen/tests/client/sidebar.test.ts`) — A view row's icon sits on the chevron column and its label runs to the right edge; a ready row's Remove is laid over the right edge on hover or focus; a failed row's Retry sits left of Remove, revealed with it; the status fades out while the actions show; Remove and Retry act on the row's view; an open pane with no views for the Project says "No screens open", and not once one is open.
- **T-F.3** (`bundles/features/mcp/tests/client/sidebar.test.ts`) — Every row's Remove is laid over its right edge on hover or focus and its status fades out; Sign in reads as a link that stays shown and steps left of Remove on hover; the pane is loading, not empty, until the watch answers, then says "No MCP servers"; a failed watch shows its alert, not the notice; the loading placeholders sit on the rows' icon column.
- **T-F.4** (versions) — covered by T-3.8.

### Phase 4 tests

- **T-4.1** (`packages/desktop/tests/panels.test.ts`) — In a rendered `FileTree`, each decorated row has a `[data-slot="tree-row-mark"]` as the last child of its `group/tree-row` wrapper, right after the button, with `sticky`, `right-1`, `shrink-0`, `pr-1`, `text-sidebar-foreground`, `peer-hover/menu-button:text-sidebar-accent-foreground` and `peer-data-active/menu-button:text-sidebar-accent-foreground`, and with no `absolute`, `w-0` or `top-*` class; it holds the letter or count. An undecorated row has none. Every wrapper is a flex row with `items-center` and without `overflow-hidden`; an open directory's `[role="group"]` has `overflow-visible`. The existing badge, description and strike cases keep passing.
- **T-4.2** (`packages/desktop/tests/panels.test.ts`) — Every row's button has `flex-1`, `min-w-0` and `pr-1.5`, and no `pr-8`.
- **T-4.3** (new `packages/desktop/tests/scroll-area.test.ts`) — `ScrollArea` without `scrollbars` and with `scrollbars="both"` renders its children in `[data-slot="scroll-area-viewport"]`, and neither puts a `scrollbars` attribute on `[data-slot="scroll-area"]`. Whether the horizontal bar mounts depends on measured overflow, which happy-dom lacks (T-4.8).
- **T-4.4** (`bundles/features/git/tests/changes-tree.test.ts`) — The repository row's line totals sit in a `[data-slot="tree-row-mark"]`, the wrapper's last child after the button, with `sticky`, `right-1` and `shrink-0`; its button has `flex-1`, `min-w-0` and `pr-1.5`, and no `pr-8`; its wrapper is a flex row without `overflow-hidden`. Once open, the repository's `[role="group"]` (`changes-tree.tsx:171`) has `overflow-visible`, and a file mark inside it is a `[data-slot="tree-row-mark"]` with `sticky`.
- **T-4.5** (`bundles/features/files/tests/client/render.test.ts`) — The Files view's `[role="tree"]` has `w-max` and `min-w-full`, and its `[data-slot="scroll-area"]` class has no `overflow-x-hidden`.
- **T-4.6** (`bundles/features/git/tests/client/render.test.ts`) — The Changes view's tree has `w-max` and `min-w-full`, and its viewport no `overflow-x-hidden`.
- **T-4.7** (versions) — `packages/bundle/tests/platform-version.test.ts` asserts `PLATFORM_VERSION` 1.6.0; `bundles/features/files/tests/manifest.test.ts` files 1.7.1 (1.6.1 before rebasing onto main's files 1.7.0); `bundles/features/git/tests/manifest.test.ts` git 1.2.2.
- **T-4.8** — Not unit-tested: whether the horizontal scrollbar appears, the marks stay pinned while scrolling, and Tab keeps `scrollLeft`. The [1:1 verification](#11-verification) covers them.

### Phase 5 tests

The class strings below are those of [§11](#11-tree-row-states) as version 9 writes them (the amendment rewrites the strings phase 5's tests pin at `e63bbccb`, [Phase 5 amendment tests](#phase-5-amendment-tests-version-9)): "the unselected fill" is `rounded-[4px]`, `hover:bg-[var(--paper-ghost-hover)]` and `active:bg-[var(--paper-ghost-pressed)]`; "the selected fill" is `rounded-[4px]` and `bg-sidebar-accent`; "the treeitem outline" is `has-[[role=treeitem]:focus-visible]:outline-2`, `has-[[role=treeitem]:focus-visible]:-outline-offset-2` and `has-[[role=treeitem]:focus-visible]:outline-[var(--sidebar-ring)]`; "the textbox outline" and "the input outline" are the same three tokens with `[role=textbox]` and `[data-slot=input]` in place of `[role=treeitem]`; "the no-fill button" has `bg-transparent`, `hover:bg-transparent`, `active:bg-transparent`, `data-active:bg-transparent` and `focus-visible:ring-0`, and none of `hover:bg-sidebar-accent`, `active:bg-sidebar-accent`, `data-active:bg-sidebar-accent`, `focus-visible:ring-2` or `hover:text-sidebar-foreground`; "the action outline set" is `focus-visible:outline-solid`, `focus-visible:outline-2` and `focus-visible:outline-[var(--sidebar-ring)]`; "keeps its ring" means the element also has `focus-visible:ring-2` and `ring-sidebar-ring` and has no `focus-visible:ring-0`. Each test reads the rendered `class` tokens; happy-dom does not compute `:hover`, `:active` or `:focus-visible`.

- **T-5.1** (`packages/desktop/tests/panels.test.ts`) — A `FileTree` rendered with decorations and `selectedPath` set to a decorated file: that row's `group/tree-row` wrapper has the selected fill and the treeitem outline, and no `hover:bg-[…]` or `active:bg-[…]` token; every other row's wrapper has the unselected fill and the treeitem outline, and no `bg-sidebar-accent`.
- **T-5.2** (`packages/desktop/tests/panels.test.ts`) — Every `[role="treeitem"]` of that tree is the no-fill button and keeps `rounded-[4px]`, `flex-1`, `min-w-0` and `pr-1.5`; the selected one keeps `data-active` and `aria-selected="true"`.
- **T-5.3** (`packages/desktop/tests/panels.test.ts`) — Each mark backing (`h-5 min-w-5`) has no `bg-sidebar/90`; on an unselected row it has `bg-sidebar`, `group-hover/tree-row:bg-[linear-gradient(var(--paper-ghost-hover),var(--paper-ghost-hover))]` and `group-active/tree-row:bg-[linear-gradient(var(--paper-ghost-pressed),var(--paper-ghost-pressed))]`; on the selected row it has `bg-sidebar-accent` and no token starting with `group-hover/tree-row:` or `group-active/tree-row:`. T-4.1's assertions on the mark's `span` keep passing.
- **T-5.4** (`bundles/features/git/tests/changes-tree.test.ts`) — The repository row's wrapper has the unselected fill and the treeitem outline; its button is the no-fill button; its mark backing has the unselected classes of T-5.3 and no `bg-sidebar/90`. The existing shape test (`changes-tree.test.ts:123-127`, `rounded-[4px]` on the button) keeps passing.
- **T-5.5** (`bundles/desktop/layout/tests/client/thread-rows.test.ts`, rendered workspace) — Plan, the selected Thread (`thread-rows.test.ts:200`), has a row wrapper with the selected fill and the treeitem outline and no `hover:bg-[…]` token; Audit's wrapper has the unselected fill and the treeitem outline and no `bg-sidebar-accent`; every row's treeitem is the no-fill button. A nested row (Form, under Plan) has the same wrapper classes, with `w-full`. Every row wrapper also has the textbox outline (D25). Version 9 (D29): Audit's plus ("New sub-thread in Audit") has `hover:bg-[var(--paper-ghost-pressed)]` and the action outline set, keeps its ring, and has no `hover:bg-sidebar-accent`. The T-2.7 case keeps passing (the shimmer). The T-2.8 case (`thread-rows.test.ts:426`) keeps its hover assertions, the elapsed badge's `group-hover/tree-row:opacity-0` and the plus's `group-hover/tree-row:opacity-100`; its assertion of the badge's `group-focus-within/tree-row:opacity-0` (`:441`) is rewritten by T-5.14.
- **T-5.6** (`bundles/features/terminal/tests/client/render.test.ts`, `TerminalRows` rendered as the case at `render.test.ts:17-65` renders it, context menu included) — Each terminal row's `[data-slot="sidebar-menu-sub-item"]` has `group/terminal`, the unselected fill and `has-[[data-sidebar=menu-sub-button]:focus-visible]:outline-2`, `…:-outline-offset-2` and `…:outline-[var(--sidebar-ring)]`. Each item holds exactly one `[data-sidebar="menu-sub-button"]`, the row's button: it has `data-slot="context-menu-trigger"`, so the outline's selector matches the button as rendered ([§11.3](#113-keyboard-focus-and-clipping)), and it is the no-fill button. No element of the rows has `data-slot="sidebar-menu-sub-button"`. Stop keeps `group-hover/terminal:opacity-100`, has `hover:bg-[var(--paper-ghost-pressed)]`, `dark:hover:bg-[var(--paper-ghost-pressed)]`, the action outline set and `focus-visible:ring-0`, and has none of `hover:bg-muted`, `dark:hover:bg-muted/50` and `focus-visible:ring-3` (version 9, D29). Rendered with `selectedId` naming one terminal, that row's item has `rounded-[4px]`, `bg-sidebar-accent` and the three outline classes and no `hover:bg-[…]` token, and its button has `data-active`; the other rows keep the unselected fill and no `data-active`. Without `selectedId` no row has `bg-sidebar-accent`. The selected row's button has `aria-current="true"`; no other row's button has `aria-current`.
- **T-5.6b** (new `bundles/features/terminal/tests/client/rows-selection.test.ts`; the rows element as the layout mounts it) — Harness, in that file:
  - happy-dom through `installWindow`, and `settle` after each change (`bundles/features/terminal/tests/client/support/render.ts:55`, `:87`);
  - the `.css` load hook of `sidebar-rows.test.ts:24-42`, registered before `elements.tsx` is imported, since that module reaches `view/terminal-view.tsx`, which imports xterm's stylesheet (`terminal-view.tsx:1`);
  - a store fake typed `TerminalStore` (`bundles/features/terminal/src/client/store.ts:52-72`) whose `terminals()` answers a list the test sets, whose `subscribe` keeps listeners the test calls after each change, and whose other methods are unused stubs; it starts with t1 and t2 under Thread A and t3 under Thread B, built like `sidebar-rows.test.ts:47-64`'s `terminal(id, threadId)`;
  - a `TerminalClient` (`elements.tsx:27-33`) of that store, with stub `api`, `watches` and `open`, passed to `defineTerminalElements` (`elements.tsx:62`); its `rows` element is defined under a fresh custom element name and mounted with `services` set and its `thread-id` property set, the way `bundles/features/mcp/tests/client/sidebar.test.ts:453-469` mounts an element;
  - a `Services` fake answering `use` and `whenAvailable` by contract id, the way `mcp/tests/client/sidebar.test.ts:379-404` does (`useService` subscribes through `whenAvailable`, `hooks.ts:13-26`): `layout.workspace/v1` with `thread(id)` answering A, B and C as root Threads of one Project (`Rows` renders nothing without this service, `elements.tsx:91-93`); `layout.tabs/v1` with `current()` answering a value the test sets and `subscribe(listener)` keeping listeners the test calls and counts, each returning a `Disposable` that removes its listener; `navigation/v1` (`packages/bundle/src/contracts/basic/navigation.ts:63`) with `current()` answering `{ params: { threadId } }` for a Thread the test sets.

  Every case below that expects no selected row first asserts that the element under A renders its two items (`[data-slot="sidebar-menu-sub-item"]`), so a mount that renders nothing cannot pass.
  - Route A, `current()` `{ id: 'terminal:t1' }`: t1's item has `bg-sidebar-accent` and its button `aria-current="true"`; t2's has neither.
  - The route set to B and the tab listeners called: A's two items render, and neither has `bg-sidebar-accent`.
  - The route set back to A and the listeners called: t1's item has `bg-sidebar-accent` again.
  - Route A with `current()` answering `{ id: 'file:a.ts' }`, then `{ id: 'terminal:' }`, then `undefined`, the listeners called after each: A's two items render each time, and neither has `bg-sidebar-accent`.
  - Mounted with a `Services` fake that has no `layout.tabs/v1`, and again with one that has no `navigation/v1`: A's two items render, and neither has `bg-sidebar-accent`.
  - The element mounted for Thread C, which has no terminal: it renders no item and the `layout.tabs/v1` fake holds no listener. The store fake then adds t4 under C and notifies: one item, and the fake holds one listener. It then removes t4 and notifies: no item, and the fake holds no listener.

- **T-5.8** (`packages/desktop/tests/tree-guides.test.ts`) — `rowInteraction(true)` returns exactly `'hover:bg-sidebar-accent active:bg-sidebar-accent'` and `rowInteraction(false)` exactly `'hover:bg-transparent hover:text-sidebar-foreground active:bg-transparent active:text-sidebar-foreground'`, so bundles built before phase 5 keep their look (D22).
