## 1:1 verification

In the running app, against the [Visual target](#visual-target):

- Sidebar column: 12 px from the search field to the first row, 12 px from the last row to New Thread, 14 px under the last pane header; New Thread 268×28 at x 6; each pane header 28 px tall.
- Three levels of Threads and terminals: icons at 6, 26 and 46 px from the row's left edge, guides at 14 and 34 px.
- Hovering and focusing an open and a closed Thread, directory and repository row: the lead icon stays.
- MCP with enough servers to pass 40% of the column: the pane stops at 40% and scrolls; the tree scrolls in the rest (SR1). With the window shortened until the Thread region reaches 40 px, New Thread stays above the pane headers and does not overlap them (SR2).
- A root Thread whose agent spawned a subagent: the parent's badge keeps counting from its own last start while the child runs, and moves when the parent wakes. Stopping the parent while the child runs leaves its shimmer and no time.
- Thread rows with the elapsed badge forced to `99:59:59` and to `99d 23h` (by a story fixture): neither badge overlaps the name's last visible letter.
- Files and Changes at the default 328 px column with a changed file eight directories deep and a repository with line totals over four digits each:
  - a horizontal scrollbar appears;
  - scrolled fully right, every name and the repository label read whole and end at least 6 px before their mark;
  - while scrolling, letters, counts and totals stay 8 px from the visible right edge, in the hover and selected colors on hovered and selected rows, vertically centered;
  - phases 1–4 only: on a decorated row the hover and selected background ends 6 px or less before the mark's left edge and does not run under the mark; on an undecorated row it reaches the tree's right end (after phase 5 the checks below replace this one);
  - Tab and Shift+Tab between rows, at scroll offsets 0, the middle and the end, leave `scrollLeft` unchanged (SR12).
- Files and Changes with no overflow: no horizontal scrollbar; each mark's right edge 8 px from the viewport's right edge and its top 4 px below its row's top, as in `files-view.png` and `changes-view.png`.
- The conversation, the branch picker and the binary-file notice show no horizontal scrollbar.
- Phase 5 ([§11](#11-tree-row-states)), in the light and the dark theme, on a root Thread row, a nested subthread row, a terminal row, a Files directory and file row, a Changes repository row and a changed file:
  - rest, hover, pressed, selected and selected under the pointer show no fill, the hover fill, the pressed fill, the selected fill and the selected fill, measured with the color picker of the developer tools against the second table of [§11](#11-tree-row-states) (version 9: `#e7e2da` and `#e2ddd4` light, `#302c28` and `#35322d` dark); the fill spans the whole row with 4 px corners, under the mark on decorated rows, and the button shows no fill of its own;
  - with a Files or Changes tree scrolled until a name runs under its pinned mark, at rest, hovered, pressed and selected, the mark backing has the row's color and hides the name, with no visible edge between mark backing and row;
  - Tab onto each row draws the row outline, a 2 px `--sidebar-ring` outline inside the row's edge with 4 px corners (version 9), with all four sides visible, nested Thread rows included, wherever the row fits its viewport; in a Files or Changes tree wider than its viewport, the row outline shows its top, bottom and the side inside the viewport (SR21); the plus of a Thread row, Stop of a terminal row, Remove and Retry of a pane row and MCP's Sign in draw their own outline when they hold focus, a 2 px `--sidebar-ring` outline outside the action (Stop's with its 1 px border inside it, [§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions)), and the row draws none;
  - an active Thread row keeps its shimmer and elapsed badge, and hover or keyboard focus still swaps the badge for the plus;
  - hovering the plus, Remove, Retry or Stop on a hovered row shows the action's square in the colors of the table in [§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions), measured with the color picker of the developer tools, in both themes; on a selected row the square shows, in the color of that table (version 9; in version 8 it was not visible).
- Phase 5, keyboard-only reveals (U14): clicking a Thread, terminal, Screens or MCP row and moving the pointer away hides its plus, Stop, Remove and Retry, shows its status again and returns Sign in to the right edge; Tab onto the row, or onto one of its actions, shows them.
- Phase 5, terminal selection: clicking a terminal row opens its tab and the row takes the selected fill, with its Thread's row; selecting another tab, closing the tab, or selecting another Thread returns it to rest. A screen reader announces the selected terminal row as current. Tab onto a terminal row draws the row outline, which confirms that `[data-sidebar=menu-sub-button]` matches the rendered button; with the pointer over Stop, Stop's hover square meets the row outline's inner edge and does not cover it ([§11.3](#113-keyboard-focus-and-clipping)). The measurement of SR22 is run and its mean recorded in the pull request.
- Phase 5, Screens and MCP: rest, hover and pressed show no fill, the hover fill and the pressed fill; Remove, Retry and Sign in show over the fill as before and each draws its action outline under Tab; Tab onto a row draws the row outline; a clicked row returns to rest once the pointer leaves; with the screen service unavailable a row does not fill. On an app built before phase 5 with screen 2.0.3 and mcp 1.2.4, the same holds (their minimal sheets), and the settings window narrower than the `md` breakpoint shows one Back control in its bar (U15, SR20).
- Phase 5, draft row: New Thread and a Thread's plus open a draft row that shows the row outline while it is open, and the hover fill under the pointer.
- Phase 5 amendment (version 9), in both themes and with the `.dark` class on the window as well as with the system dark setting alone: Stop's hover square is the color of the table of [§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions) and not `--muted`; a Files or Changes row scrolled under its mark shows no edge between the mark backing and the row at rest, hovered and pressed; with forced colors on, Tab onto a row and onto each action still draws the outline. Forced colors are turned on with the developer tools' Rendering panel ("Emulate CSS media feature forced-colors: active"), which works on every OS the app runs on, and also, on a Windows machine, with a Windows contrast theme; macOS has no forced-colors mode of its own. The action outlines draw at all (the `focus-visible:outline-solid` of [§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions)): Tab onto the plus, Remove, Retry, Stop and Sign in shows a 2 px outline whose color the developer tools' color picker reads as `--sidebar-ring`, in both themes. The contrast ratios are not measured here: SR19 computes them. On an app built before phase 5, with layout 3.0.2 and neither git 1.2.3, terminal 1.1.3, screen 2.0.3 nor mcp 1.2.4 active, Tab onto the plus shows its 2 px `--sidebar-ring` ring (SR16, D29).
- Phase 5, rename (D25): double-clicking a Thread's name, and a terminal row's context-menu Rename, each draw the row outline from the moment the field opens until Enter, Escape or a click elsewhere closes it, which confirms in Electron that both fields, and the draft's field, match `:focus-visible` when opened by the mouse; if one does not, the fallback of [§11.3](#113-keyboard-focus-and-clipping) applies before phase 5 merges, and this check is repeated with it; the terminal's input shows its border and no ring of its own; the Thread's field keeps its underline.
- Follow-up of 2026-11-11: Tab onto a project list row on `/projects`, a project grid card and a comment pin; each draws a visible focus outline (2 px `--paper-focus` on the project buttons, 2 px `outline-ring` on the pin).

## Deferred to the plan

The stage 4 review's Nits, left to the plan by the user's decision (2026-11-10). All are resolved (2026-11-10): U10's wording and SR6's "layout 1.0.0" are fixed in this spec; the others were resolved in the plan's "Deferred nits, resolved" table.

- T-1.1 should assert the rem values `indentation` returns (`0.375rem`, `1.625rem`, `4.125rem`), not px.
- U10's "background spans the full scrolled width" reads against D16 (the background ends at the mark); align the wording.
- Name and type the `ThreadBranches` prop that carries the activity map.
- SR6's "layout 1.0.0" means `layout.sidebar/v1` 1.0.0.
- T-1.2 does not cover the rename field's `TerminalIcon` lead slot (§8).
- G3 lists `isAgentBusy`, which has no unit test of its own.
- `useThreadRows` lives in `bundles/desktop/layout/src/client/hooks/use-thread-rows.ts`.
- Place the 14 px bottom spacing (on `SidebarContent` or the last pane).
- §1: `executionStartedAt` falls back to `updatedAt` when `lastStarted` names another prompt (`threads.ts:134-139`).
- Docs to update are not assigned to phases.
- §3 and SR4: cite the follow's other effects (`output-watch.ts:155-160`, a toast during render).
- The breadcrumb shows an empty label for an Untitled Thread (`thread-breadcrumb.tsx:118`, `:131`).
- §4 misses a caller of `threadIconKind`: `bundles/desktop/layout/tests/sidebar.test.ts:25`, `:195-203`.
- §7 misses other `0.75rem` uses (chat styles; papercraft `styles.css:17`).
- §2: `threadLevel` excludes orphan Threads.
- Line ranges to correct: `workspace.ts:105-125`, `contracts/index.ts:331-420`, `bundles/features/screen/src/client/index.ts:35-45`.
- §10.2 (version 8: phase 5 gives them one, [§11](#11-tree-row-states)): unselected rows have no hover background (`rowInteraction(false)` is `hover:bg-transparent`, `tree-guides.tsx:19-22`), and the repository row is always unselected (`changes-tree.tsx:104`); these lines are at `bb4c2453`, as the rest of this list.
- §9.3: the height chain passes through react-resizable-panels' inner `div`.

## Open questions

Open: none.

Closed:

- **SQ1 — Pane header height.** Closed (2026-11-10, the user): the Screens and MCP pane headers stay 28 px (`h-7`). The captures draw them 20 px; Figma is being updated to match, and this spec's text is the reference.
- **SQ2 — `aria-expanded` on Thread rows.** Closed (2026-11-10, the user): Thread rows with children get `aria-expanded`, `true` while open and `false` while closed; rows without children have none ([§6](#6-lead-icon-without-hover-swap), T-1.9).
- **SQ3 — Other sidebar rows.** Asked: should the Screens and MCP pane rows and the draft row take the same hover, pressed and focus states? Closed (2026-11-11, the user): yes. None of them has a selection, so none has a selected state ([§11.6](#116-pane-rows-and-the-draft-row), D24).
- **SQ4 — A selected terminal row.** Asked: should a terminal row show the selected fill while its terminal is open? Closed (2026-11-11, the user): yes, with the terminal rows taking the other states too. A terminal row is selected while its Thread is the active tab owner and its selected tab is the terminal's ([§11.4](#114-terminal-rows-and-row-actions), D23).
- **SQ5 — A ring on rows being renamed.** Asked: should a Thread or terminal row being renamed draw the row outline, as the draft row does? Closed (2026-11-11, the user): yes ([§11.3](#113-keyboard-focus-and-clipping), D25).
- **SQ6 — Ring on wide Files rows.** Asked: in a Files or Changes tree wider than its viewport, a focused row's ring loses the side outside the viewport ([§11.3](#113-keyboard-focus-and-clipping), SR21); is that acceptable? Closed (2026-11-11): the user did not answer on the stage 2 or stage 3 review, so the default holds: yes, no mitigation in phase 5.
- **SQ7 — The pane header's plus.** Asked: U14 moves row reveals to keyboard focus, while the Screens and MCP pane headers reveal their plus on any focus (`project-sidebar.tsx:155`), so a header clicked with the mouse keeps its plus shown; should it follow U14? Closed (2026-11-11): no answer, so the default holds: no; it is a header, U14 names rows, and `project-sidebar.tsx` does not change in phase 5.
- **SQ8 — The terminal rename input's own ring.** Asked: D25 removes the `Input`'s 3 px focus ring so the row outline is the only one, and the input keeps its border in `--ring` ([§11.3](#113-keyboard-focus-and-clipping)); the preview did not show it; keep it so? Closed (2026-11-11): no answer, so the default holds: yes.

- **SQ9 — A listed sheet instead of a scanned one.** U15 records minimal sheets that scan the screen and mcp client sources. The cascade check of [§11.6](#116-pane-rows-and-the-draft-row) found that the scanned mcp sheet re-emits `border`, `transition-transform` and `hover:text-foreground` after rules that win today on a chat comment line, the thread rail under reduced motion and a dark-theme tab, and that both scanned sheets leave 101 pairs to judge on every change. Each sheet now lists its bundle's 14 or 15 phase 5 classes instead (`@source inline(…)`, about 4.8 KB each). The check, rerun at stage 4 with the specificity of each compiled selector and with the elements as phase 5 leaves them, finds ten pairs per sheet and no conflict: the stage 3 run had missed the `data-open:hover:bg-sidebar-accent` pairs on the row buttons and one `aria-expanded` pair, none of which changes a rendered element. Keep the lists? Default: yes. The other answer is the scan with every conflicting class excluded through `@source not inline(…)`, an exclusion list that must follow the classes of every other bundle in the window. Closed (2026-11-11, the user): keep the listed sheets ([§11.6](#116-pane-rows-and-the-draft-row)).

- **SQ10 — The light theme's focus outline fails WCAG 2.2 1.4.11.** Closed (2026-11-11, the user): answer (b), `outline-[var(--sidebar-ring)]`, for the rows and, by U17, for the row actions. The question as asked: the approved outline color, `--paper-focus` `#a89b86`, has 2.36:1 against `--sidebar`, 2.20:1 against `--sidebar-accent`, 2.12:1 against the hover fill and 2.02:1 against the pressed fill in the light theme; the criterion asks 3:1 (SR19). The dark theme passes (8.12:1 or more). Options, each changing only the third token of every row outline set (`outline-[…]`) in the files, the screen and mcp lists and the tests that name it, a cost D22 repeats in each of those places ([§11](#11-tree-row-states), "What a token choice costs"):
  - (a) Keep `--paper-focus`, as approved. The light theme's row focus stays below 3:1.
  - (b) `outline-[var(--sidebar-ring)]`. Light `#8a7f70` (`tokens.css:38`, equal to `--paper-blue-2`): 3.40:1, 3.16:1, 3.04:1, and 2.90:1 against the pressed fill, which shows only while the pointer is held down. Dark `#d9cdb6`, the same color as `--paper-focus` (`tokens.css:133`, `:160`), so the dark theme does not change. It is version 8's ring color. Recommended by this spec: the smallest change that meets 3:1 for a row focused from the keyboard with no pointer on it.
  - (c) `outline-[var(--paper-blue-3)]`. Light `#776c5e`: 4.44:1, 4.13:1, 3.98:1, 3.80:1; dark `#e2d6bf`: 11.15:1, 10.04:1, 9.63:1, 8.88:1. Meets 3:1 in every state; darker than the Dashboard's focus mark.
  - (d) Change the light value of `--paper-focus` in `packages/desktop/src/tokens/tokens.ts:115` (and the generated `tokens.css`), for example to `#8a7f70`. It also fixes the Dashboard and every other user of the token, and is a theme change outside this spec.

  Applied: `outline-[var(--sidebar-ring)]` is the third token of every ring set of [§11](#11-tree-row-states) (including the `:focus` fallback of D25) and of the action outline set, in the screen and mcp lists of [§11.6](#116-pane-rows-and-the-draft-row) (4 604 and 4 766 bytes compiled) and in T-5.1, T-5.4–T-5.6, T-5.9–T-5.13; the cascade check was rerun (§11.6). Contrast: SR19. U17 added the row actions after the question was asked: inside an action's outline, while the pointer is on the action, `--sidebar-ring` has 2.50:1 to 2.71:1 against the action's hover square in the light theme; every row and action focused from the keyboard with no pointer on it has 3.16:1 or more.

- **SQ11 — The hover fill differs from `--sidebar` more than the selected fill.** With the paper tokens, a hovered row differs from `--sidebar` more than a selected row does (light 1.12:1 against 1.07:1, dark 1.16:1 against 1.11:1; pressed 1.17:1 and 1.26:1), in both themes. The selected fill is unchanged by the user's decision (`bg-sidebar-accent`, no hover fill on a selected row). Options: (a) keep it, as approved; (b) give the selected fill a color that differs from `--sidebar` more than the pressed fill does, which the user would choose on a preview and which needs its own mark-backing check. Closed (2026-11-11, the user): (a), kept (U17). The paper palette's selected-item token, `--paper-thumbnail-selected`, was checked and is not used: 1.04:1 against `--sidebar` in the light theme, below the hover fill's 1.12:1, and 1.30:1 in the dark theme ([§11](#11-tree-row-states), [Alternatives considered](#alternatives-considered)).
